Method: 提出排列不变微调（PI-FT），在数据加载时对每条记录随机采样字段顺序并随机丢弃字段，使编码器学习标签语义而非位置信息。同时构建DevDataBench，完全由LLM生成覆盖15种语言、所有指标的地面真值查询，用于训练与评估。

Result: 标准微调在字段顺序改变时nDCG@10下降7.4点，PI-FT仅损失0.2点；118M参数CPU模型经PI-FT训练后，以0.707 nDCG@10超越text-embedding-3-large的0.556，在低资源语言上增益最大。

Conclusion: 字段顺序是结构化元数据检索中被忽视的关键因素，PI-FT可低成本消除位置依赖；合成查询生成可替代缺失的用户日志；小模型经针对性微调在检索任务上优于通用大模型零样本性能，为AI代理访问公共数据提供了可自部署的解决方案。

Abstract: We study retrieval over catalogs of structured metadata, where each record is a small schema whose fields answer different kinds of query. Embedding a record with a text encoder first serializes its fields into a string, which forces a choice of field order. We show this choice, usually treated as an implementation detail, silently controls retrieval quality once the encoder is fine-tuned. A standard fine-tune loses 7.4 nDCG@10 points when the index is rebuilt under a different field order, because it reads absolute position instead of the field labels. We propose permutation-invariant fine-tuning ($\textbf{PI-FT}$), which serializes each record under a freshly sampled field order with random field dropout, so meaning binds to the labels rather than to position. The change is about two lines in the data loader; it costs negligible in-distribution accuracy and cuts the order-change penalty to 0.2 points. We study this in the discovery of development statistics, a catalog of nearly 10,000 indicators that should be searchable in many languages by a model small enough to self-host. As AI assistants and agents increasingly mediate access to public data and statistics, this retrieval step decides whether an answer is grounded in the right indicator or series, making discoverability a precondition for disseminating data through AI. Because usage logs cannot provide training signal for indicators no one has searched, we generate the queries instead. $\textbf{DevDataBench}$ is a fully LLM-generated benchmark of grounded, facet-targeted queries across 15 languages, covering every indicator for both training and evaluation. A fine-tuned 118M-parameter CPU encoder outperforms every zero-shot baseline, including $\texttt{text-embedding-3-large}$ (0.707 vs.\ 0.556 nDCG@10), with the largest gains in low-resource languages. We release the benchmark, pipeline, models, and a reusable PI-FT framework.

</details>


### [111] [SIMAX: A Scalable and Interpretable Framework for Multi-Fidelity and Annotated Clinician-Patient Dialogue Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30491)
*Zhuhan Bao,Rui Yang,Bohao Yang,Zhiyi Liu,Sicheng Shu,Ruio Heerschap,Le Li,Doris Yang,Elisabeth Bond,Haoyuan Wang,Nicoleta Economou-Zavlanos,Joshua M. Biro,Matthew McDermott,Nan Liu,Anand Chowdhury,Kai Sun,Kathryn Pollak,Ed Hammond,Chuan Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: SIMAX是一个用于生成可控医患对话数据并附带行为标注的框架，通过自动化和人工评估验证了其质量，可为临床沟通编码系统的开发、验证和优化提供数据基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI驱动的临床沟通编码系统需要真实对话数据和人工标注标签进行评估，但这些数据难以大规模获取且成本高昂、不一致性高，亟需一种可扩展的受控对话数据生成方法。

Method: 提出SIMAX框架，从预定义的临床场景、角色人设、语音条件和目标沟通行为生成医患对话，使用Global Codebook（整体沟通质量）和WISER Codebook（特定可计数行为）两个编码本控制行为，并通过自动化和人工质量评估以及下游沟通编码系统示例进行验证。

Result: 生成3,388条模拟对话，覆盖3个专科、多种就诊阶段、角色特征和口音条件。自动化评估：UTMOS 3.03、WV-MOS 2.61、WER 0.07、CER 0.05、CLAP余弦相似度0.41；人工评估：MOS中位数4.67、临床真实感中位数3.00。下游评估表明SIMAX可评估编码系统对不同行为目标的响应并揭示部分维度的敏感性不足。

Conclusion: SIMAX能够生成可控且可复现的模拟医患对话，为临床沟通编码系统的开发、验证和优化提供了可靠的数据基础。

Abstract: Background. The widespread deployment of ambient digital scribes is driving large-scale capture of clinician-patient dialogues. Human coding of clinical communication data remains costly, inconsistent, and difficult to scale, motivating AI-driven communication coding systems. However, evaluating these systems requires real-world dialogues and human-coded labels, both hard to obtain at scale.
  Methods. We developed SIMAX (Scalable and Interpretable Framework for Multi-Fidelity and Annotated Clinician-Patient Dialogue Simulation), a framework for generating controlled clinical dialogue data with reference behavioral annotations. SIMAX generates clinician-patient dialogues from predefined clinical scenarios, personas and voice conditions, and target communication behaviors. Behaviors are controlled using two codebooks: the Global Codebook for overall communication quality and the WISER Codebook for specific countable behaviors. We evaluated SIMAX using automated and human quality assessments and an example communication coding system.
  Results. SIMAX generated 3,388 simulated dialogues across three specialties, multiple visit stages, persona characteristics, and accent conditions. Automated assessment showed mean UTMOS and WV-MOS scores of 3.03 and 2.61, WER and CER of 0.07 and 0.05, and CLAP cosine similarity of 0.41, suggesting reasonable speech naturalness, high transcription fidelity, and positive text-audio correspondence. Human evaluation showed a median MOS of 4.67 and a median clinical realism score of 3.00. Downstream evaluation suggests that SIMAX can assess how a communication coding system responds to behavioral targets and reveal insufficient sensitivity in some dimensions.
  Conclusions. SIMAX generates controlled and reproducible simulated clinician-patient dialogues, providing a data foundation for developing, validating, and refining communication coding systems.

</details>


### [112] [Regime-Aware Peer Specialization for Robust RAG under Heterogeneous Knowledge Conflicts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30518)
*Bo Wang,Heyan Huang,Yaolin Li,Yanghao Zhou,Jiahao Teng,Ziyi Yang,Ge Shi,Chong Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RAPS-DA提出了一种面向检索增强生成中异构冲突的领域感知专家特化框架，通过样本级三领域（Grounding/Arbitration/Resistance）硬路由分配和令牌级双层选择器过滤不稳定令牌，在不依赖更强教师模型的情况下显著提升RAG鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）中，外部检索上下文与模型参数化知识之间的冲突具有异构性（涵盖可靠、部分可靠到对抗性证据），现有方法采用跨领域无关的监督方式，将不同可靠性领域的不兼容学习信号混为一谈，导致模型难以有效处理这些冲突。

Method: 提出RAPS-DA框架，包含两个粒度：(1) 样本级：将冲突划分为Grounding、Arbitration、Resistance三个领域，每个领域从共享基座模型训练一个同规模专家，并将样本硬路由到匹配的专家进行on-policy反向KL监督；(2) 令牌级：双层选择器利用教师间不一致性、学生-教师分歧和学生熵，过滤无信息或不稳定令牌，对置信度错配令牌加权，随学生成熟逐步聚焦高冲突令牌。

Result: 在五个冲突场景和两个分布外基准上，RAPS-DA超越了所有提示、解码、微调、强化学习和单教师基线方法。

Conclusion: 增益来自固定模型规模的专家特化，而非更强教师；专家仅在训练时存在，部署学生无需领域标签或专家访问，实现了高效且鲁棒的RAG。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves language models by grounding generation in external context. However, it can be fragile when the retrieved context conflicts with the model's parametric knowledge. Such conflicts span a reliability spectrum, ranging from reliable and partially reliable evidence to adversarial context. Existing remedies often handle such heterogeneous conflicts with regime-agnostic supervision, which can conflate incompatible learning signals across reliability regimes. To disentangle these signals, we propose RAPS-DA, a regime-aware peer specialization framework that addresses conflict at two complementary granularities. At the sample level, conflicts are divided into three regimes, including Grounding, Arbitration, and Resistance, with one same-scale peer specialist trained per regime from a shared base model. Each sample is then hard-routed to its regime-matched peer for on-policy reverse-KL supervision. At the token level, a dual-layer selector uses inter-teacher disagreement, student-teacher divergence, and student entropy to filter uninformative or unstable tokens, upweight confidently misaligned ones, and gradually focus supervision on high-conflict tokens as the student matures. Gains stem from specialization at a fixed model scale, not from a stronger teacher, and the peer specialists exist only during training, so the deployed student requires no regime labels or peer access. Experiments on five conflict scenarios and two out-of-distribution benchmarks show RAPS-DA surpasses all prompting, decoding, fine-tuning, RL, and single-teacher baselines.

</details>


### [113] [TRACE: Temporal Relationship-Aware Conversational Entrainment Detection in Dyadic Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30543)
*Sathvik Manikantan Napa Ugandhar,Hao Zhang,Alison Gunzler,Yuzhe Wang,Thomas Thebaud,Georgi Tinchev,Venkatesh Ravichandran,Laureano Moro-Velázquez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出DyadEE数据集（用于双人语音交互中的情感同步检测）和TRACE框架（基于窗口级声学嵌入建模对话交互轨迹），在情感同步检测任务上达到97.01%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着语音AI智能体的普及，理解对话交互中的情感同步（emotional entrainment）变得日益重要。情感同步受社会关系和对话语境影响，但缺乏专门用于检测该现象的数据集和建模方法。

Method: 构建DyadEE数据集，包含真实情感同步对话和通过伙伴交换与情感重合成破坏同步的合成交互。提出TRACE框架，将双人交互建模为基于情感微调Whisper表征的窗口级有序声学嵌入序列，将每个样本视为交互轨迹而非聚合话语。

Result: 在DyadEE数据集上，融入对话上下文和关系信息可提升情感同步检测性能，TRACE框架达到97.01%的最佳准确率。

Conclusion: 对话上下文和关系信息对情感同步检测至关重要，TRACE的窗口级轨迹建模方式优于传统聚合方法。

Abstract: With the proliferation of speech AI agents, understanding emotional entrainment in conversational interaction has become increasingly important. Emotional entrainment is shaped by social relationships and conversational context, influencing affective coordination over time. We introduce DyadEE, a dataset for emotional entrainment detection in dyadic speech interactions, containing both emotionally entrained conversations and synthetic interactions where entrainment is disrupted through partner swapping and emotion resynthesis. We further propose TRACE, a window-level framework that models dyadic interaction as ordered sequences of acoustic embeddings derived from emotion fine-tuned Whisper representations, treating each sample as an interaction trace rather than pooled utterances. Experimental results on DyadEE show that incorporating conversational context and relationship information improves emotional entrainment detection, with TRACE achieving the best accuracy of 97.01%.

</details>


### [114] [Poller: Are LLMs Suitable for Evaluating the Poetry Understanding Task?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30556)
*Shanshan Wang,Derek F. Wong,Jingming Yao,Lidia S. Chao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Poller（Poetry LLM Evaluator）方法，通过让大语言模型扮演诗人角色来评估现代诗歌理解任务，实验表明该方法在多个维度上显著降低了评估误差，尤其在修辞技巧和陌生化维度上分别实现了94.55%和89.53%的误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动评估方法不适用于现代诗歌因其独特的文学本质，人工评估虽可靠但成本高且无法应用于大规模数据，因此需要一种既高效又可靠的自动化诗歌评估方法。

Method: 提出Poller方法，要求LLM扮演具有详细信息的诗歌作者角色，通过采用诗人的视角来模拟人类评估和判断，从而在八个专业维度上评估诗歌理解任务。

Result: 在多个LLM上的实验表明，Poller方法有效降低了LLM与人类之间的评估误差，尤其在修辞技巧维度达到94.55%的误差降低，在陌生化维度达到89.53%的误差降低。

Conclusion: 该方法弥合了自动化效率与人类专业性之间的差距，为诗歌相关任务的自动化评估奠定了基础，且验证了其在多个LLM和多个维度上的有效性。

Abstract: Traditional automatic evaluation methods have been shown to be unsuitable for modern Chinese poetry because of the distinct nature of this literary genre. Human evaluation remains reliable, but is expensive and not applicable to large-scale data. In this paper, we propose Poller (Poetry LLM Evaluator), a novel method leveraging large language models (LLMs) to evaluate the poetry understanding task. Specifically, our method requires LLMs to play the role of a poem's author with detailed information, thereby emulating human evaluation and judgment by adopting the poet's perspective. We conducted comprehensive experiments on multiple LLMs, evaluating the interpretations of poems across eight specialized dimensions. Experimental results demonstrate that our method effectively reduces the evaluation error between LLMs and humans. Especially for specific dimension evaluation, Poller-based LLMs achieve a 94.55% and 89.53% error reduction for rhetorical techniques and defamiliarization, respectively, compared to baseline methods. These performances are unattainable by conventional LLM evaluation methods. Experimental results from multiple LLMs across various dimensions validate the efficacy of our method. This work bridges the gap between automated efficiency and human expertise, establishing a foundation for automated evaluation in poetry-related tasks.

</details>


### [115] [Morphing into Hybrid Attention Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30562)
*Disen Lan,Jianbin Zheng,Yuxi Ren,Xin Xia,Xuanda Wang,Xuefeng Xiao,Xipeng Qiu,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出FlashMorph方法，将混合层选择建模为预算约束的子集优化问题，通过可学习门控和线性化正则化在合成长上下文检索数据上优化，高效实现Transformer到混合注意力架构的转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合注意力模型中层选择方法依赖启发式策略（如固定布局或逐层评分），将层重要性视为独立个体，忽略了全局混合配置下各层之间的相互依赖效应。

Method: 1) 将混合层选择形式化为预算约束的子集优化问题；2) 构建可变形模型，为每个全注意力层配备转换后的线性注意力分支；3) 冻结模型权重，在合成长上下文检索数据上联合优化逐层门控，使用线性化正则化促进模型依赖线性注意力；4) 学习到的门控在预设全注意力预算下离散化以实例化混合架构；5) 进行标准logits蒸馏和长上下文微调。

Result: FlashMorph能发现更有效的混合配置，在保持强长上下文召回和通用基准性能的同时，显著降低层选择成本，证明了其在有效性、效率和可扩展性方面的优势。

Conclusion: 将混合层选择作为全局优化问题处理比启发式方法更有效，FlashMorph提供了高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Hybrid attention models improve long-context efficiency by retaining only a subset of full-attention layers and replacing the remaining layers with linear attention. However, the effectiveness of Transformer-to-hybrid conversion critically depends on which layers preserve full attention. Existing hybrid layer selection methods typically rely on heuristic strategies such as fixed placement patterns or layerwise scoring, implicitly treating layer importance as isolated and overlooking the interdependent layer effect under a global hybrid configuration. In this work, we formulate hybrid layer selection as a budget-constrained subset optimization problem. We further propose FlashMorph (Fast LAyer Selection for Hybrid MORPHing), an effective, efficient and scalable layer selection method for Transformer-to-hybrid conversion. FlashMorph first constructs a morphable model by equipping each full-attention layer with a converted linear-attention branch. It then freezes all model weights and jointly optimizes layerwise gates on synthetic long-context retrieval data, with a linearization regularization that encourages the model to rely on linear attention for efficiency. The learned gates are discretized under a preset full-attention budget to instantiate the hybrid architecture, followed by standard logits distillation and long-context finetuning. Extensive experiments show that FlashMorph discovers more effective hybrid configurations, preserves strong long-context recall and general benchmark performance while substantially reducing layer selection cost compared with existing layer selection methods, demonstrating its effectiveness, efficiency, and scalability.

</details>


### [116] [Uncertainty-Aware Generation and Decision-Making Under Ambiguity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30578)
*Nico Daheim,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并评估了基于贝叶斯决策理论和风险厌恶决策的不确定性感知决策算法，应用于辅导和自动同行评审任务，发现贝叶斯方法优于风险厌恶规则，但需谨慎实施以避免生成通用输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在复杂真实任务中广泛应用，但许多任务具有高度主观性且需要可信输出，而决策算法方面的研究相对不足。

Method: 基于贝叶斯决策理论和风险厌恶决策，将辅导策略和评审分数的不确定性纳入考量，并使用保形预测提供策略和分数的保证。

Result: 贝叶斯方法表现更优，风险厌恶规则在歧义高时可能退化（生成通用输出），算法需谨慎实施。

Conclusion: 决策理论技术可以改进LLM决策，但存在开放挑战，风险厌恶规则需注意通用输出问题。

Abstract: With rapidly improving capabilities, Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used in many complex real-world tasks. Beyond requiring in-depth knowledge and reasoning skills, many of these tasks exhibit a high degree of subjectivity and require that the outputs of the model can be trusted. While a lot of progress has been made to train better models, decision-making algorithms have received less attention. In this work, we present and evaluate various uncertainty-aware decision-making algorithms based on Bayesian decision theory and risk-averse decision making on the tasks of tutoring and automatic peer reviewing. Concretely, we take uncertainty over tutoring strategies and review scores into account when generating a tutor response or review and use conformal prediction to provide guarantees over strategy and score. We find empirically that these algorithms can improve the utility of the generations but need to be carefully implemented when ambiguity is high. For example, risk-averse rules can degrade performance by optimizing for generic outputs, while Bayesian methods tend to perform better. Our work uses techniques from decision theory to improve LLM-based decision-making and outlines open challenges for the community.

</details>


### [117] [Scaling the Horizon, Not the Parameters: Reaching Trillion-Parameter Performance with a 35B Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30616)
*Lei Bai,Zongsheng Cao,Yang Chen,Zhiyao Cui,Shangheng Du,Yue Fan,Shiyang Feng,Zijie Guo,Haonan He,Liang He,Xiaohan He,Shuyue Hu,Yusong Hu,Songtao Huang,Yichen Jiang,Hao Li,Xin Li,Dahua Lin,Weihao Lin,Fenghua Ling,Dongrui Liu,Zhuo Liu,Runmin Ma,Chunjiang Mu,Haoyang Peng,Tianshuo Peng,Jinxin Shi,Luohe Shi,Boyuan Sun,Zelin Tan,Shengji Tang,Qianyi Wang,Yiming Wu,Yi Xie,Xiangchao Yan,Jingqi Ye,Peng Ye,Fangchen Yu,Jiakang Yuan,Bihao Zhan,Bo Zhang,Chen Zhang,Shufei Zhang,Shuaiyu Zhang,Wenlong Zhang,Yiqun Zhang,Junpeng Zhao,Zhijie Zhong,Bowen Zhou,Yuhao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Agents-A1，一个35B参数的MoE智能体模型，通过扩展智能体视野（agent horizon）达到万亿参数级别的性能。通过构建长视野知识-动作基础设施（平均45K token轨迹），并采用三阶段训练策略（全领域SFT、领域级教师模型、多教师领域路由在线蒸馏），在多个长视野基准上超越或达到Kimi-K2.6、DeepSeek-V4-pro等万亿参数模型的水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何通过扩展智能体视野（包括长视野轨迹的规模化和异构智能体能力的规模化），使得较小参数量的模型（35B）能够达到万亿参数级别模型的性能，从而提供一种更高效的模型扩展路径。

Method: 1）构建长视野知识-动作基础设施，生成平均45K token的智能体轨迹；2）三阶段训练：全领域监督微调对齐基础模型、领域级教师模型捕获专业能力、多教师领域路由在线蒸馏（含显著词汇对齐）提升跨领域知识迁移效率，统一六个异构领域到一个可部署学生模型。

Result: 在SEAL-0（56.4）、IFBench（80.6）、HiPhO（46.4）、FrontierScience-Olympiad（79.0）、MolBench-Bind（56.8）上取得领先；在SciCode（44.3）、HLE（47.6）、BrowseComp（75.5）上保持强竞争力。

Conclusion: 35B参数的Agents-A1可以通过扩展智能体视野，在长视野任务上达到或匹配万亿参数模型的性能，为社区提供了一条实用的模型扩展路径。

Abstract: We introduce Agents-A1, a 35B Mixture-of-Experts Agentic Model that reaches trillion-parameter-level performance by scaling the agent horizon. We investigate agent-horizon scaling from two perspectives: scaling long-horizon trajectories and scaling heterogeneous agent abilities. To support this goal, we build a long-horizon knowledge-action infrastructure that connects external knowledge, actions, observations, and verifier outcomes, producing agentic trajectories with an average length of 45K tokens. Based on this, we train Agents-A1 with a three-stage recipe. First, we perform full-domain supervised fine-tuning to align the base model with broad agentic behaviors. Second, we train domain-level teacher models to capture specialized expertise in each domain. Third, we propose a multi-teacher domain-routed on-policy distillation with salient vocabulary alignment to improve knowledge transfer efficiency across different domains, unifying six heterogeneous domains into one deployable student model. Agents-A1 achieves strong and broad performance for long-horizon agent benchmarks. Compared with 1T-parameter model such as Kimi-K2.6 and DeepSeek-V4-pro, Agents-A1 achieves leading results on SEAL-0 (56.4), IFBench (80.6), HiPhO (46.4), FrontierScience-Olympiad (79.0), and MolBench-Bind (56.8), and remains highly competitive on SciCode (44.3), HLE (47.6) and BrowseComp (75.5). We hope this work provides the community with a practical path for scaling the horizon using a 35B agent that can reach or match the performance of 1T models on long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [118] [ReFreeKV: Towards Threshold-Free KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2502.16886)
*Xuanfan Ni,Liyan Xu,Chenyang Lyu,Longyue Wang,Mo Yu,Lemao Liu,Fandong Meng,Jie Zhou,Piji Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出现有KV缓存压缩方法依赖输入特定的阈值来分配预算，这限制了其在开放域场景中的鲁棒性。作者提出"无阈值"目标，并首次实现为ReFreeKV方法，能够自适应分配预算而保持全缓存性能。在13个数据集上验证了其有效性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KV缓存剪枝方法需要针对输入/领域预设特定的阈值才能达到最优性能，这种依赖输入敏感阈值的设计在开放域场景中因输入多样性（领域、长度、难度各异）而难以适用，导致在任意输入上出现显著性能下降。

Method: 提出"无阈值"（threshold-free）目标，即自适应调整预算分配同时保持全缓存性能。ReFreeKV是该目标的首次实例化实现。

Result: 在13个覆盖不同上下文长度、任务类型和模型规模的数据集上验证了ReFreeKV的有效性和效率。

Conclusion: ReFreeKV作为首个无阈值KV压缩方法，通过自适应预算分配解决了传统方法对输入敏感阈值的依赖问题，在多样化的开放域输入上实现了鲁棒的性能。

Abstract: To reduce memory consumption during LLM inference, a handful of methods have been proposed for KV cache pruning. While these techniques can accomplish lossless memory reduction on many datasets, they often hinge on an under-emphasized condition: an input/domain-specific threshold for KV cache budget needs to be pre-determined to achieve the optimal performance. However, such input-sensitive design may be considerably limited in real-world scenarios, as open-domain inputs span diverse domains, lengths and difficulty levels, without clear boundaries for threshold selection. As a result, the dependence of such input-sensitive threshold can be a fundamental limitation that causes large degradation on arbitrary inputs. In this work, we propose a new objective that lifts the threshold constraints for robust KV compression, advocating for "threshold-free" methods that adaptively adjust budget allocation while preserving full-cache performance. We then propose a novel method, ReFreeKV, serving as the first instantiation of this objective. Extensive experiments across 13 datasets with diverse context lengths, task types, and model sizes demonstrate its efficacy and efficiency. Our code is publicly released at https://github.com/Patrick-Ni/ReFreeKV.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Concurrent Splay-Based Tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28889)
*Vitaly Aksenov,Rene van Bevern,Artem Shilkin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于并发二叉搜索树的类Splay旋转设计，通过基于访问计数的双深度阈值控制节点旋转，在保持倾斜工作负载性能优势的同时减少根附近竞争，并给出了两种变体（64位精确计数器和6位近似计数器），实验证明该方法能在多种倾斜工作负载下提升吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并发有序索引数据结构主要关注最坏情况保证，但实际工作负载中对象访问分布往往不均匀（如Zipf分布）。虽然顺序场景下有高效的分布自适应数据结构（如Splay Tree），但这些结构在并发场景下难以高效实现。Splay Tree虽能应对倾斜工作负载，但每次访问将节点旋转到根会引发根附近的高竞争问题。

Method: 提出一种类Splay的旋转设计，不将访问节点移至根，而是使用基于静态最优性复杂度的两个深度阈值：仅在节点深度显著超过上阈值时旋转，且旋转在到达下阈值前停止。该设计包含两种变体：一种是每个节点使用64位精确访问计数器，另一种使用6位近似计数器。该旋转设计基于Bronson等人的并发AVL树实现。

Result: 在顺序只读树上证明了静态最优性；实验表明，在多种倾斜工作负载下，该方法相比基线能够提升系统吞吐量。

Conclusion: 通过双阈值旋转机制，在保留Splay Tree对倾斜工作负载优势的同时，有效降低了根节点附近的竞争，从而提升了并发场景下的整体性能。

Abstract: Most work on efficient concurrent ordered indices, such as concurrent binary search trees, B-trees, skip lists, etc., has focused on data structures that provide good \emph{worst-case} guarantees. In real workloads, objects are often accessed at different rates, since access distributions may be non-uniform. Many efficient distribution-adaptive data structures exist in the sequential case; however, they are often complicated to make efficient in the concurrent case.
  The most prominent distribution-adaptive data structure is Splay Tree. Its most important advantage is that it does not store any balancing information and provides a reasonable performance improvement on extremely skewed workloads, such as Zipfian workloads. This paper proposes a splay-like rotation design for concurrent binary search trees. Instead of moving an accessed node to the root, rotations use two depth thresholds that are based on the static-optimality complexity computed from the number of accesses to the node: a node is rotated only when it is substantially deeper than the upper threshold, and rotations of the node stop before reaching the lower threshold. This design aims to preserve the main practical benefit of splaying on skewed workloads while reducing contention near the root.
  We present two variants of the rotation design: one using an exact 64-bit access counter per node and one using a 6-bit approximate counter. We prove static optimality for the corresponding sequential read-only tree and evaluate both rotation designs by implementing them on top of the concurrent AVL tree of Bronson et al. Our experiments show that the approach can improve throughput on several skewed workloads.

</details>


### [120] [Five Ways to Build a Concurrent Linked From Coarse-Grain Locking to Lock-Free Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28972)
*Zeeshan Mohammed Rangrej*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了五种并发链表实现（从粗粒度锁到无锁设计），在C++中实现并测试了不同读写负载和链表规模下的性能，发现最佳算法取决于使用场景：粗粒度锁和惰性链表在读密集型和小键范围场景表现最佳，无锁链表在大键范围和高线程数时具有竞争力，而细粒度锁因节点级锁开销过大性能最差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多线程环境下链表并发访问的正确性与效率问题需要系统性的对比研究，以指导实际应用中的算法选择。

Method: 实现了五种并发链表（粗粒度锁、细粒度锁、乐观锁、惰性链表、无锁链表），在C++中测量不同工作负载（读密集、平衡、写密集）和不同链表规模下的吞吐量。

Result: 粗粒度锁和惰性链表在读密集型和小键范围场景下最优；无锁链表在大键范围和高线程数时表现好；细粒度锁因节点级锁开销过高，在所有场景中性能最差。

Conclusion: 并发链表的最佳算法选择高度依赖使用场景，不存在适用于所有情况的通用最优方案。

Abstract: Linked lists are one of the most basic data structures in computer science. But when many threads try to use the same linked list at the same time, things get complicated. In this paper, we look at five different ways to make a linked list work correctly and efficiently with multiple threads running at once. We start with the simplest approach -- one big lock for the whole list -- and step by step improve it, ending with a lock-free design that uses no locks at all. We implemented all five versions in C++ and measured how fast each one is across different workloads (read-heavy, balanced, and write-heavy) and different list sizes. Our results show that the right choice of algorithm depends heavily on how the list is used: the coarse-grain and lazy lists win under read-heavy workloads with small key ranges, while the lock-free list becomes competitive when key ranges are large and more threads are running. Fine-grain locking, despite its theoretical appeal, pays a heavy cost from per-node lock overhead and consistently performs the worst in our tests.

</details>


### [121] [Importance-Aware Resource Allocation for Collaborative Task-Oriented Semantic Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29052)
*Kaiyi Lei,Yuanzhe Peng,Letian Zhang,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出 iCoTASC 框架，通过离线预计算效用查找表与在线轻量级查表调度，实现多设备协作语义通信中面向任务的实时信道自适应资源分配，无需重训练推理模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务导向语义通信方案要么在线计算开销过大，要么依赖端到端重训练与慢变信道假设，无法满足快速衰落信道与严格端到端延迟约束下的实时资源分配需求。

Method: 提出 iCoTASC 混合框架：1) 利用归因重要性引导逐维度嵌入选择作为通信控制信号；2) 通过数据驱动效用函数建模量化带来的语义收益递减；3) 离线预计算各发射机的效用查找表，在线仅需查表与低复杂度调整即可实现调度。

Result: 实现分布式系统中实时、信道自适应的语义资源分配，无需重训练底层任务推理模型，且在线计算复杂度低。

Conclusion: iCoTASC 通过离线-在线混合设计，在保证任务性能的同时显著降低在线调度计算开销，适用于时变信道下的多设备协作语义通信场景。

Abstract: Task-oriented semantic communication must allocate scarce radio resources to semantic features under fast fading wireless conditions and strict end-to-end latency budgets. Existing solutions are either optimization-heavy, leading to prohibitive computational overhead during online operation, or rely on end-to-end retraining procedures together with slowly varying channel assumptions. We propose iCoTASC (importance-aware Collaborative Task-Oriented Semantic Communication), a hybrid offline-online framework designed for collaborative multi-device semantic communication systems. iCoTASC leverages attribution-based importance to guide per-dimension embedding selection as a practical communication control signal, models diminishing semantic returns of quantization through a data-driven utility function, and precomputes per-transmitter utility lookup tables offline, which together enable lightweight online scheduling via table lookup and low-complexity refinement under time-varying channels. The proposed framework supports real-time, channel-adaptive semantic resource allocation in distributed systems without requiring retraining of the underlying task inference model.

</details>


### [122] [Are There Manufacturer Differences in Hard-Drive Reliability?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29078)
*Christoph Siemroth,Yeomyung Park*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 基于Backblaze HDD数据集，使用两种持续时间回归模型分析四大硬盘制造商（HGST、Seagate、Toshiba、WD）的中短期故障率差异。在控制年龄、容量、外形尺寸和温度后，发现东芝故障率略高于希捷，HGST故障率最低（约为希捷的41%），WD故障率约为希捷的52%。同时记录了年龄、容量、温度和位置对故障率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究四大硬盘制造商在中短期故障率上是否存在显著差异，为数据中心采购和存储系统可靠性管理提供实证依据。

Method: 使用Backblaze HDD数据集，采用两种不同的持续时间回归模型，控制驱动年龄、容量、外形尺寸和温度等变量，比较HGST、Seagate、Toshiba和WD四家制造商的故障率差异。

Result: 东芝故障率略高于希捷；HGST故障率最低，约为希捷的41%；WD故障率显著高于HGST，约为希捷的52%。年龄、容量、温度和位置对故障率有显著影响。

Conclusion: 不同制造商HDD的中短期故障率存在显著差异，HGST可靠性最优，WD次之，Seagate和Toshiba相对较高；控制变量对故障率有重要影响。

Abstract: Based on the Backblaze hard disk drive (HDD) dataset, we analyze whether the four major HDD manufacturers represented in the dataset -- HGST, Seagate, Toshiba, Western Digital (WD) -- show differences in short- to medium-term HDD failure rates. Using two different duration regression models, we find -- holding constant drive age, capacity, form-factor, and drive temperature -- that Toshiba's failure rate is slightly above Seagate's. HGST HDD failure rates are the lowest, about 41% of Seagate's. WD HDD failure rates are significantly above HGST's, but still only about 52% of Seagate's. We also document the effects of age, capacity, temperature and drive location on failure rates.

</details>


### [123] [FBench: A Flexible Benchmark for CFG-Based What-If Exploration of HPC I/O Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30197)
*Zhaobin Zhu,Chen Wang,Kathryn Mohror,Sarah Neuwirth*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FBench是一种基于CFG的代码透明基准测试工具，通过Recorder轨迹生成配置文件或实时重放I/O模式，支持POSIX/MPI-IO接口和优化注入，能准确复现不同优化和文件系统设置下的I/O行为，大幅减少假设分析时间并实现最高8倍性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模HPC应用的I/O性能受访问模式、中间件优化和文件系统配置的复杂交互影响。反复运行完整应用进行探索成本高昂，因此需要一种灵活、代码透明的工具来高效进行假设分析（what-if analysis）和I/O性能探索。

Method: 利用Recorder轨迹导出的上下文无关文法（CFG）生成简化全局配置文件用于基准执行，或在无需额外预处理的情况下实时重放I/O模式。支持POSIX和MPI-IO接口，允许用户通过JSON配置文件注入优化提示，实现无需修改代码的I/O设置快速实验。

Result: IOR和HACC-IO中精确匹配扩展行为和Lustre条带参数敏感性；FLASH Sedov中揭示Lustre上集合I/O写带宽比独立I/O低30倍，切换到突发缓冲文件系统使非集合写带宽提升约1.5倍；LAMMPS中显著减少假设分析时间，通过简单调优实现最高8倍性能提升。

Conclusion: FBench能准确复现合成负载和真实负载的I/O行为，捕获不同优化和文件系统设置下的访问模式和性能趋势，是一种高效的HPC I/O假设分析工具。

Abstract: The I/O performance of large-scale HPC applications depends on a complex interplay of access patterns, middleware optimizations, and file system configurations. To systematically explore these effects without repeatedly rerunning full applications, we introduce FBench, a flexible and code-transparent benchmarking tool for what-if analysis and I/O performance exploration. FBench leverages context-free grammars (CFGs) derived from Recorder traces to either generate simplified global configuration files for benchmark execution or replay I/O patterns on-the-fly without additional preprocessing. It supports both POSIX and MPI-IO interfaces and allows users to inject optimization hints via JSON configuration files, enabling rapid experimentation with I/O settings without code changes. Our evaluation shows that FBench accurately reproduces I/O behavior for both synthetic and real workloads, capturing access patterns and performance trends across diverse optimizations and file system settings. For IOR and HACC-IO, FBench closely matches scaling behavior and sensitivity to Lustre striping parameters. For FLASH Sedov, it reveals that collective I/O on Lustre can yield up to 30x lower write bandwidth than independent I/O, largely independent of striping, and that switching to a burst buffer file system increases non-collective write bandwidth by about 1.5x without additional tuning. The evaluation with LAMMPS shows that FBench can significantly reduce the time required for what-if analyses and, with simple tuning, enable improvements of up to 8x.

</details>


### [124] [KernelFlume: Elastic Core-Attention Scaling for Agentic Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29207)
*Guangyu Xiang,Xueze Kang,Lin Zhang,Wenxiang Lin,Shaohuai Shi,Yuxin Wang,Xiaowen Chu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: KernelFlume提出了一种面向解码阶段的解耦架构，将投影/FFN计算与核心注意力计算分离：权重节点执行密集投影/FFN核，而无权重的注意力节点存储token范围的KV分区并随请求状态需求弹性扩展。通过路由表、UCX端点通信和查询优先的核心注意力调度等技术，在动态长上下文代理工作负载下，相比ServerlessLLM实例级弹性扩展，p99 TPOT保持在~74ms（A6000）和~34ms（H100），成本降低32%-61%，模拟大规模场景下成本降低56-66%，异构硬件下可达80-85%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统在处理突发性长上下文解码工作负载时，采用实例级弹性扩展方式（启动完整模型副本）来增加KV容量，导致启动延迟高、内存开销大和批次碎片化问题，亟需更细粒度的弹性扩展方案。

Method: 1) 解耦架构：将投影/FFN路径（权重节点）与核心注意力计算（注意力节点）分离；2) 弹性路由：路由表映射token范围到注意力节点端点，在token边界更新路由，利用主机可见图信号驱动UCX端点通信；3) 延迟优化：查询优先的核心注意力调度与层间核流水线结合，重叠远程注意力/通信与本地投影/FFN计算。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B动态长上下文代理工作负载下，A6000上p99 TPOT ~74ms，H100上~34ms；成本相对ServerlessLLM降低32%（A6000）和61%（H100）；大规模模拟成本降低56-66%，异构硬件下可达80-85%，可扩展到百万token上下文范围。

Conclusion: KernelFlume通过解耦投影/FFN与注意力计算，实现了比传统实例级弹性扩展更高效、更低成本的LLM服务架构，尤其适合动态长上下文解码场景。

Abstract: LLM serving is increasingly dominated by long and dynamic decode workloads from agents, reasoning models, and extended conversations. When bursty long-context demand exceeds deployed capacity, existing serving systems typically scale out by launching additional serving instances with model replicas. This instance-level elasticity increases KV capacity only by provisioning another full copy of the model, inheriting startup latency, memory overhead, and batch fragmentation.
  We present KernelFlume, a decode-centric architecture that disaggregates the stable projection/FFN path from core-attention computation: weight nodes execute dense projection/FFN kernels, while weightless attention nodes store token-range KV partitions and scale with request-state demand. To make this separation elastic, KernelFlume maintains a routing table that maps token ranges to attention-node endpoints. It updates routes at token boundaries and uses host-visible graph signals to drive pre-registered UCX endpoint communication outside the captured CUDA Graph. To preserve low per-token latency after disaggregation, KernelFlume combines query-first core-attention dispatch with inter-layer kernel pipelining, overlapping remote attention and communication with local projection/FFN work. On real GPU testbeds (intra-node A6000 and cross-node H100), under a dynamic long-context agentic workload serving Llama-3.1-8B, KernelFlume sustains flat p99 TPOTs of ~74 ms on A6000 and ~34 ms on H100, while lowering cost per million output tokens by up to 32% and 61%, respectively, relative to full-instance elastic scaling with ServerlessLLM, a state-of-the-art instance-startup method. Replaying the same trace at larger model scale in simulation projects a 56--66% cost reduction over ServerlessLLM, widening to 80--85% with cheaper heterogeneous attention-node hardware and persisting into the million-token context range.

</details>


### [125] [Fog Computing and Large Language Models: A vision for the mutual beneficiaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29483)
*Satish Narayana Srirama*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了雾计算与大语言模型（LLMs）之间的双向赋能关系：雾计算为LLM提供近端计算基础设施以解决其云中心化带来的延迟和隐私问题，同时LLM通过代码生成能力增强雾计算的动态应用部署能力，并综述了相关优化技术及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM通常依赖云中心，面临与云中心化物联网相同的延迟、网络负载和隐私问题，促使将LLM部署到近端雾基础设施中；同时雾计算也需要LLM的代码生成能力来支持动态应用部署，二者存在相互赋能的必要性。

Method: 通过综述当前雾计算与LLM交叉领域的研究现状，分析LLM在雾端部署所需的优化技术（如参数量化、剪枝、低秩适配等），以及LLM对雾计算应用部署的增强作用。

Result: 指出雾计算与LLM可以相互受益：雾计算为LLM提供近端计算环境以克服云中心化缺陷，LLM的代码生成能力可促进雾应用的动态部署。

Conclusion: 雾计算与LLM之间存在双向赋能关系，当前已有初步优化方案，但该领域仍存在广阔的未来研究空间。

Abstract: Fog computing utilizes proximal computational resources for sensor data processing and actuation, and addresses the latency, network load, and privacy issues of cloud-centric Internet of Things. On the other hand, Large Language Models (LLMs) are a type of deep learning AI models, which are trained on enormous text data, that perform various natural language processing tasks such as translation, question answering, text summarization, and code generation. LLMs are generally cloud-centric, requiring abundant GPU memory and computing capabilities, again face the same issues that led to fog computing. This pushes the necessity for LLM support in the proximity on fog infrastructure, requiring LLM optimizations such as parameter-weight quantization, pruning, low-rank adaptation etc. Meanwhile, fog computing also gets benefit from LLM's ability for code generation, in the dynamic deployment of fog-based applications. The paper addresses how both fog computing and LLMs can be mutual beneficiaries, discussing the state-of-the-art and future research scope.

</details>


### [126] [Energy-Efficient Multimodal Inference Serving with Tri-serve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29629)
*Ziyang Jia,Sara Rashidi Golrouye,Laxmi Bhuyan,Benjamin Kubwimana,Devashree Tripathy,Zexin Li,Cong Liu,Daniel Wong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Tri-serve提出了一种软件DVFS控制器，通过解决硬件PMU导致的三种功率低效问题（级间依赖停顿、自动升频与算术强度反相关、热节流），在commodity GPU上实现22%能效提升且不影响延迟或吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU内置电源管理单元（PMU）自动进行频率升频/降频决策，但这些硬件管理的频率决策会导致显著的功率低效，造成能源浪费。

Method: 提出Tri-serve，一种软件级DVFS控制器，联合考虑三种功率低效类别：级间依赖停顿（Dependency stalls）、算术强度对频率和功率的影响（Arithmetic-intensity effect）、高热算强度阶段的热节流效应（Thermal-throttling effect）。

Result: 在commodity GPU上实现22%能效提升，且对延迟或吞吐量无负面影响。

Conclusion: 通过软件层面的DVFS控制，可以有效纠正硬件PMU的次优频率决策，在不牺牲性能的前提下显著提升多模态推理服务的能效。

Abstract: Multimodal model inference creates substantial energy demand with growing performance requirements. Within GPUs, power is autonomously managed by an on-board power management unit (PMU), which makes frequency boosting/throttling decisions. However, we find that these hardware-managed frequency decisions can cause significant power inefficiency.
  This work identifies three classes of power inefficiencies within modern multimodal inference serving: (1) inter-stage dependency stalls run at near maximum frequency despite being idle; (2) anti-correlation between auto-boost frequency and arithmetic intensity (A.I.) results in compute-bound phases (e.g., prefill) running at lower frequency and vice versa; and (3) thermal throttling degrades SM frequency and throughput.
  We propose Tri-serve, a software-based DVFS controller that jointly accounts for three classes of inefficiency -- inter-stage Dependency stalls, the Arithmetic-intensity effect on frequency and power, and the Thermal-throttling effect of high A.I. phases -- to deliver energy-efficient multimodal serving on commodity GPUs. We show that Tri-serve achieves 22% energy efficiency improvement with no latency or throughput impacts.

</details>


### [127] [NI-ORCA: A Parallel Algorithm for Counting the Orbits of Non-Induced Graphlets up to K4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29651)
*Syed Ibtisam Tauhidi,Arindam Karmakar,Thai Son Mai,Hans Vandierendonck*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: NI-ORCA是一种并行算法，用于高效计算网络中大小为四以内的非诱导图元轨道计数，通过三角形计数、4-团枚举和轨道求解三阶段设计，结合阶段特定的并行化策略，在真实和合成数据集上实现最高30倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法虽然能高效计算诱导图元的轨道，但许多实际应用需要非诱导轨道计数，而当前尚无方法能在精确、可扩展且并行的层面上支持非诱导轨道计数。

Method: NI-ORCA扩展了ORCA框架用于非诱导轨道计数，通过重新表述线性方程组来实现。算法包含三个阶段：三角形计数、4-团枚举和轨道求解。采用线程和顶点局部内存模型及数据结构设计了阶段特定的并行化策略，最小化竞争和平衡工作负载。分析了调度策略、块大小和亲和策略对性能的影响。

Result: 在八个真实世界数据集和一系列合成Erdős–Rényi图上的实验表明，结合阶段特定数据结构与动态调度及小块大小的混合模式能实现一致的加速比和有效的负载均衡。NI-ORCA显著优于最先进的串行算法，实现高达30倍加速。

Conclusion: NI-ORCA首次实现了非诱导图元轨道计数的精确、可扩展并行计算，验证了阶段特定并行化策略在解决该问题上的有效性。

Abstract: Counting the orbits of graphlets in a network is a vital tool for understanding the structural roles of vertices in various graph analytics tasks. While existing algorithms efficiently compute orbits of induced graphlets, many real-world applications require non-induced orbit counts. However, no current method offers exact, scalable, and parallel support for non-induced orbit counting. This paper presents NI-ORCA, a parallel algorithm to efficiently compute the orbits of non-induced graphlets up to size four (4-clique). NI-ORCA extends the ORCA framework for non-induced orbit counting by reformulating a system of linear equations. The algorithm consists of three stages: triangle counting, 4-clique enumeration, and orbit solving. We design and implement stage-specific parallelisation strategies using thread and vertex-local memory models and data structures, minimising contention and balancing workload. We further analyse the impact of scheduling policies, chunk sizes, and affinity strategies on performance. Experimental analysis on eight real-world datasets and a series of synthetic Erddos-Renyi graphs demonstrates that a mixed mode combining stage-specific data structure, with dynamic scheduling with small chunk sizes, delivers consistent speedup and effective load balancing. Our results show that NI-ORCA significantly outperforms state-of-the-art sequential algorithms, achieving up to 30x speedups.

</details>


### [128] [Demystifying the Design Space and Best Practices for Heterogeneous LLM Inference and Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29708)
*Zhixin Wang,Zhengbo Wang,Fangcheng Fu,Yinhui Lu,Jinlong Hou,Yijie Chen,Xiaowei Shen,He Liu,Xiangbin Li,Jun Chen,Ruya Gu,Dian Wang,Zhou Tan,Yuan Cheng,Hongzhou Zhang,Xiangjun Huang,Ping Zhang,Xiaohe Hu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文系统分析了异构PD推理中跨配置的设计空间，提出四个设计轴（加速器、精度、互连、KV驻留），并识别出三个关键边界决策（计算放置、KV表示、KV所有权），指出精度策略应分属运行时角色而非统一设置，KV传输应关注字节语义而非张量语义，以及KV生命周期需要显式所有权管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异构PD推理部署各自独立决策，缺乏跨配置的整体视角，不清楚哪些决策必须在PD边界处联合做出、哪些可以独立做出，导致系统设计缺乏系统性指导。

Method: 沿四个设计轴（加速器、精度、互连、KV驻留）构建设计空间，分析各轴间的交互作用，识别在异构PD推理中成为约束条件的边界决策。

Result: 识别出三个关键边界决策：计算放置、KV表示、KV所有权。发现精度策略应分属运行时角色而非统一设置；KV传输本质是字节移动而非张量语义；KV生命周期的预订、释放、故障恢复跨越PD两端，需要显式所有权管理。

Conclusion: 异构PD推理中仅部分轴间交互成为约束条件，通过三个边界决策可提供具体指导。跨厂商和互连相关的主张基于工业部署观察和运行时源代码审查给出设计指导。

Abstract: Heterogeneous prefill-decode (PD) inference is now in production: prefill on cost-efficient or supply-available accelerators, decode on bandwidth-strong ones, and KV state crossing mixed interconnects in mixed numerical formats. Each deployment makes these decisions on its own. What is missing is the picture across configurations-which decisions must be made jointly at the PD boundary, and which can be made independently. We propose a design space organized along four design axes-accelerator, precision, interconnect, and KV residency and the workload regime (stage pressure) they respond to. We show that only a subset of interactions among these factors become binding constraints once PD inference becomes heterogeneous. These interactions surface through three recurring boundary decisions: compute placement, KV representation, and KV ownership. The resulting analysis yields concrete guidance. Precision policy belongs to runtime roles rather than to a single system-wide setting, because the same low-bit format relieves different bottlenecks on each side of the boundary. KV transfer engines move bytes rather than tensor semantics, making representation compatibility an explicit boundary concern whenever producer and consumer differ. The KV handoff also carries a lifecycle-reservation, release, and failure recovery-that spans prefill and decode and requires explicit ownership. Two further interactions remain open. Cross-vendor and interconnect-related claims are stated as design guidance grounded in industrial deployment observations and source-code inspection of the runtimes involved.

</details>


### [129] [SMART-MIG: A Learning Framework for Scalable and Energy-Efficient GPU Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29775)
*Wenqing Yu,Neel Karia,Tanvi Hisaria,Clifford Stein,Olivier Tardieu,Asser Tantawi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SMART-MIG是一个结合均值场多智能体强化学习（MF-MARL）与启发式调度算法的并行计算系统，用于大规模GPU MIG动态分区调度，在保持算法复杂度恒定的同时，实现能效与时延效率的优化，相比静态分区方案提升18%的能效-时延效率，且能耗仅比理论下界高27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIG技术允许在GPU分区上运行小型ML模型以提高利用率和降低能耗，但现有方案在兼顾能效与时延效率、保持可管理的算法复杂度方面面临巨大挑战，特别是在大规模调度中需要同时决定GPU分区方式和作业调度方案。

Method: 提出SMART-MIG系统，核心包含两部分：1) 基于均值场多智能体强化学习（MF-MARL）的大规模MIG重分区组件，其复杂度随作业和GPU数量增长保持恒定；2) 针对作业调度的定制化启发式算法。

Result: SMART-MIG相比静态分区方案提升了18%的能效-时延效率，能耗仅比理论下界高27%，且重分区组件的复杂度保持恒定。

Conclusion: SMART-MIG有效解决了大规模MIG调度中能效与时延效率的权衡问题，通过MF-MARL实现恒定复杂度的动态分区，结合启发式调度算法，显著优于静态分区方案，并接近理论最优性能。

Abstract: The emergence of Multi-Instance GPU (MIG) technology enables us to run smaller machine learning models on partitions of a GPU rather than the entire device, thus improving utilization and reducing energy consumption, albeit with potential performance trade-offs. Meanwhile, the growing energy demands of GPU-equipped data centers motivate the development of online partitioning and scheduling schemes that not only ensure fast job processing but also achieve high energy efficiency. However, achieving energy-tardiness efficiency with manageable algorithmic complexity in large-scale scheduling remains a great challenge, due to the dual objectives of deciding on the GPU partitions and scheduling jobs onto the slices of the heterogeneous partitions. To address this challenge, we propose SMART-MIG, a parallel computing system that combines Mean-Field Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MF-MARL) for large-scale MIG repartitioning with tailored heuristic algorithms for job scheduling. We demonstrate that the complexity of the repartitioning component remains constant even as the number of jobs and GPUs increases. We also establish theoretical lower bounds on energy consumption and tardiness to rigorously benchmark system performance. Finally, extensive experiments show that SMART-MIG improves the energy-tardiness efficiency by $18\%$ compared to its corresponding static-partitioning counterpart, while being only $27\%$ above the theoretical lower bound on energy consumption.

</details>


### [130] [Beyond Uniform Experts: Cost-Aware Expert Execution for Efficient Multi-Device MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29982)
*Hui Zang,Pengfei Xia,Hong Liu,Jiajia Chu,Tuo Hao,Minghao Chen,Rui Zhang,Ziyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CAEE提出了一种硬件感知的运行时框架，通过轻量级成本模型预估硬件开销，选择性剪枝低贡献高成本的专家并引入低开销补偿机制，在671B DeepSeek-R1模型上实现8%-18%的端到端推理延迟降低，同时保持模型准确率下降小于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构中，低贡献专家与高贡献专家在内存和传输成本上几乎一致，导致成本效益比低下；同时多设备部署受限于最慢设备，单设备局部优化难以转化为端到端延迟提升。

Method: CAEE框架包含：(1) 轻量级校准成本模型预测硬件开销；(2) 基于token级专家重要性和成本的联合优化，选择性剪枝低重要性的高成本专家；(3) 低开销补偿机制重新分配被剪枝专家的贡献，避免额外数据移动。

Result: 在671B DeepSeek-R1模型上，CAEE在专家卸载和多设备本地执行等场景下，实现8%-18%的端到端推理延迟降低，且模型准确率下降小于1%。

Conclusion: 通过硬件引导的运行时优化，CAEE有效解决了MoE推理中数据移动瓶颈问题，在保持模型质量的同时显著提升推理效率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures enable language models to achieve unprecedented scale via sparse activation. However, their inference performance is often limited by data movement bottlenecks. Two coupled challenges exacerbate this limtation: (1) Importance-Agnostic Cost: Low-contribution experts incur nearly uniform memory and transfer costs, resulting in a low cost-to-benefit ratio and wasting critical bandwidth; (2) System-Level Imbalance: Multi-device deployments are universally bottlenecked by the slowest device, meaning that local reductions on one device may yield no improvement in end-to-end latency. We propose Cost-Aware Expert Execution (CAEE), a hardware-guided runtime framework that jointly optimizes for token-level expert importance and system-level execution cost. CAEE uses lightweight, calibrated cost models to estimate hardware overhead, selectively prunes low-importance, high-cost experts, and redistributes their contributions via a low-overhead compensation mechanism, avoiding extra data movement. Evaluations on the 671B DeepSeek-R1 model show that CAEE can reduce end-to-end inference latency by 8\%-18\% across diverse deployment settings, including expert offloading and on-device execution on multi-device systems, while maintaining a model accuracy drop of less than 1\%.

</details>


### [131] [Energy-Aware Scheduling for Serverless LLM Serving on Shared GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30391)
*Tianyu Wang,Gourav Rattihalli,Aditya Dhakal,Longfei Shangguan,Dejan Milojicic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Festina是一个面向无服务器LLM推理服务的节能控制平面，通过协调请求调度、GPU资源分配和能效优化，在满足SLO的前提下，相比现有系统最多节省56%的能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理已成为主要的云工作负载，其能源足迹日益增长，亟需集群级别的能源优化。然而，无服务器LLM服务的多模型共享GPU资源特性使得节能变得困难——共存的多个模型在同一个设备级工作点下运行，而其资源需求和延迟容忍度在不同执行阶段和负载条件下动态变化，现有系统缺乏有效的节能调度机制。

Method: Festina采用"全局调度器+局部调度器+工作负载整合"的三层协同架构。全局调度器基于离线profiling和GPU状态摘要，通过常数时间查询实现SLO安全、能效感知的请求放置；局部调度器在每块GPU上根据执行阶段动态调整任务批处理和计算资源；并通过SLO感知的迁移实现能效驱动的工作负载整合，减少GPU静态功耗。

Result: 与四种SOTA LLM服务系统及一种DVFS增强系统相比，Festina在SLO达标率（2%以内）相当的前提下，最多降低56%的能耗。

Conclusion: 通过全局-局部协同的能效感知调度，以及请求放置、运行时资源适配和工作负载整合的联合优化，可以在满足TTFT/TBT SLO的前提下显著降低无服务器LLM服务的集群能耗。

Abstract: As LLM inference becomes a major cloud workload, its growing energy footprint makes cluster-wide energy optimization increasingly important. Serverless LLM serving helps platforms absorb traffic volatility by elastically sharing GPU resources across models, but this sharing also makes energy optimization difficult. Multiple co-resident models run under one device-wide operating point, while their resource demands and latency slack change across execution phases and load conditions. As a result, minimizing energy requires coordinated scheduling across request placement, runtime resource adaptation, and workload consolidation.
  We present Festina, a profiling-guided, power-aware control plane to minimize cluster-wide energy for serverless LLM serving. Unlike common global-local schedulers that focus on throughput or tail latency, Festina makes energy-first decisions by jointly coordinating request placement, SM partitioning, and GPU operating points under TTFT/TBT SLOs. In our system, a lightweight global scheduler performs fast, SLO-safe, energy-aware placement using constant-time lookups from offline profiles and GPU state summaries. On each GPU, a phase-aware local scheduler continuously adapts task batching and compute resources to minimize power consumption. Festina further performs energy-aware workload consolidation to reduce GPUs' static power consumption via SLO-aware migration. Comparison with four SOTA LLM serving systems and one DVFS-augmented system demonstrates that Festina reduces energy consumption by up to 56% while maintaining parity in SLO attainment (within a 2% margin)

</details>


### [132] [Analyzing Linearizability in Relativistic Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30419)
*Kahbod Aeini,Wojciech Golab*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文基于Gilbert和Golab的相对论线性化理论，正式证明了复制状态机和ABD读写寄存器构造在相对论框架下的线性化性质，其证明强度高于Jayanti的核心定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爱因斯坦相对论表明时间是相对的，受动力学和引力膨胀影响，分布式系统的执行不能简单地按墙钟时间全序建模。Gilbert和Golab提出了相对论线性化概念但未给出形式化正确性证明，Jayanti最近指出了这一点。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 运用Gilbert和Golab提出的相对论线性化理论工具，对复制状态机以及Attiya、Bar-Noy和Dolev（ABD）提出的读写寄存器构造的变体进行形式化分析，建立其相对论线性化性质。

Result: 成功建立了复制状态机和ABD变体的相对论线性化性质，且证明强度高于Jayanti核心定理所达到的水平。

Conclusion: Gilbert和Golab的理论工具能够有效地证明异步算法的相对论线性化性质，且能达到比现有结果更强的正确性保证。

Abstract: Einstein's theory of relativity correctly predicted that time is relative, and subject to both kinematic and gravitational dilation. Therefore, executions of distributed systems cannot always be modeled as sequences of events totally ordered according to wall clock time. To address this fundamental problem, Gilbert and Golab formulated a generalization of Herlihy and Wing's linearizability property for shared objects, which they called \emph{relativistic linearizability}, and introduced a collection of theoretical tools to facilitate rigorous analysis. While they conjectured that several widely-studied classically linearizable algorithms are also relativistically linearizable, their work stopped short of presenting formal proofs of correctness, as pointed out recently by Jayanti. In this paper, we explain how Gilbert and Golab's techniques can be used to establish relativistic linearizability for a replicated state machine, as well as variations of the widely studied read/write register construction of Attiya, Bar-Noy and Dolev (ABD). Our results establish a stronger form of relativistic linearizability than Jayanti's central theorem for these asynchronous algorithms.

</details>


### [133] [GPU Parallelization Strategies for Forward and Backward Propagation in Shallow Neural Networks: A CUDA-Based Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30497)
*Rania Zitouni,Nadine Bousdjira,Sarah Hasnaoui,Amel Sadoun,Fatma Salhi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文比较了三种CUDA优化策略（平铺共享内存+列填充消除存储体冲突、预转置权重矩阵实现合并全局内存访问、融合MatMul+ReLU核函数减少中间内存往返）在前向和反向传播中的应用。在NVIDIA Tesla T4上，全优化版本相比基线CUDA在大数据集上实现1.41倍加速（21.0s→14.8s）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究CUDA优化策略对浅层神经网络前向和反向传播性能的影响，通过系统比较三种优化技术来提升GPU加速深度学习原语的效率。

Method: 在浅层神经网络中堆叠应用三种CUDA优化：(1) 使用+1列填充的平铺共享内存消除存储体冲突；(2) 预转置权重矩阵实现全局内存的合并访问；(3) 融合MatMul+ReLU核函数消除中间全局内存往返。实验在NVIDIA Tesla T4上进行，对比数据集大小分别为小、中、大三组。

Result: 全优化实现在大数据集（25,600样本）上相比基线CUDA版本获得1.41倍加速比，执行时间从21.0s降至14.8s。与顺序CPU基线和OpenMP并行实现相比，GPU优化版本展现出显著的性能优势。

Conclusion: 内存访问优化（包括共享内存存储体冲突消除、全局内存合并访问以及核函数融合）是提升GPU加速深度学习训练性能的关键因素，三种优化策略的组合效果优于单一优化。

Abstract: We present a comparative study of CUDA optimization strategies applied to forward and backward propagation in a shallow neural network. Three stacked optimizations are evaluated: (1) tiled shared memory with bank-conflict elimination via +1-column padding, (2) pre-transposed weight matrices for coalesced global memory access, and (3) a fused MatMul+ReLU kernel that eliminates intermediate global-memory round-trips. Experiments on an NVIDIA Tesla T4 (CUDA 13.0) across three dataset sizes show that the fully optimized implementation achieves a 1.41x speedup over the baseline CUDA version on the large dataset (25,600 samples), reducing execution time from 21.0s to 14.8s. Results are compared against a sequential CPU baseline and an OpenMP parallel implementation, demonstrating the effectiveness of memory-access optimization in GPU-accelerated deep learning primitives.

</details>


### [134] [Spandana: Reconciling Strict SLOs with Low Cost under Fine-Grained Load Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30533)
*Dilina Dehigama,Shyam Jesalpura,Zeyu Xu,Marton Nemeth,Shengda Zhu,Marios Kogias,Boris Grot*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Spandana通过将请求在VM和FaaS层之间动态调度，解耦了SLO保障与成本优化，在满足严格SLO的同时实现了76-86%的CPU利用率和5-44%的成本降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云服务面临亚秒级负载波动，现有弹性伸缩、无服务器计算及混合系统无法同时满足严格SLO、低成本和高利用率的要求。

Method: 提出Spandana架构，包含轻量级SLO控制器（按请求粒度在VM与FaaS间路由）和资源分配器（考虑VM成本、FaaS成本和流量波动性优化VM资源配置）。

Result: 严格满足SLO，CPU利用率达76-86%，相比三种基线方法成本降低5-44%。

Conclusion: 通过解耦SLO执行与成本优化，Spandana有效解决了细粒度负载波动下SLO合规与成本效率之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Cloud-based online services face significant sub-second load fluctuations while needing to meet strict Service Level Objectives (SLOs). Cluster operators often over-provision resources to protect SLOs, sacrificing utilization and cost efficiency. Existing reactive and proactive autoscalers, serverless (FaaS) deployments, and VM/FaaS hybrid systems fail to reconcile strict SLO compliance with low cost and high utilization under fine-grained load fluctuation.
  We introduce Spandana, an architecture that addresses this trade off by decoupling SLO enforcement from cost optimization. A lightweight controller colocated with each application VM enforces SLOs by steering each arriving request between the VM and FaaS. Requests that can meet the SLO stay on the VM; the remaining requests are forwarded to a stock FaaS layer such as AWS Lambda. For cost optimization, Spandana's resource allocator determines the most-efficient VM provisioning by accounting for VM cost, FaaS cost, and traffic volatility, allowing the VM pool to run at high utilization. Our evaluation shows that Spandana maintains strict SLO adherence, achieves 76-86% CPU utilization, and reduces cost by 5-44% over three SOTA baselines.

</details>


### [135] [Data Replication Meets Function Scheduling in the Edge-Cloud Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30563)
*Matteo Cenzato,Dario d'Abate,Arianna Dragoni,Matteo Briscini,Alessandro Margara*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对Serverless计算在边缘-云连续体中的状态管理问题，研究了在强一致性和最终一致性两种模型下的函数调度与数据放置联合优化问题，提出了全局贪婪启发式算法和聚合视图启发式算法，实验表明数据放置比函数调度对结果影响更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Serverless计算在边缘-云连续体中具有吸引力，但无状态假设在处理持久数据时失效：从远端云存储获取状态会消除边缘运行的低延迟优势。保持数据靠近需要复制，而复制又强制了与函数执行位置和应用程序所需一致性耦合的放置决策。

Method: 首先将问题建模为二元线性规划（BLP）求最优解作为参考基准；然后提出两种启发式算法：Global-View贪心方法（利用完整全局信息）和Aggregated-View启发式（每个节点仅根据本地观察需求独立决策）。

Result: Global-View启发式与最优解相差不到几个百分点，且可扩展到超过10^4节点；Aggregated-View启发式牺牲部分解质量但能持续适应每次调用；在客户端移动场景下，集中式策略因快照过时和延迟峰值而表现不佳，而Aggregated-View保持低且稳定的客户端延迟。

Conclusion: 在所有实验中，数据放置比函数调度对最终结果的影响更大。

Abstract: Serverless computing is an appealing model for the edge-cloud continuum, but its stateless assumption breaks down once functions need persistent data: fetching state from a distant cloud store erases the latency benefit of running at the edge. Keeping data close means replicating it, and replication forces a placement decision that is coupled with where functions execute and with the consistency each application demands. We study this joint problem of function scheduling and data placement under two consistency models, strong and eventual replication. We first formulate it as a Binary Linear Program that yields the optimal placement for a given system snapshot, and use it as a reference point. Because the solver does not scale past a few hundred nodes, we add two heuristics with progressively less information: a Global-View greedy method that works from the same complete snapshot, and an Aggregated-View heuristic in which each node decides from locally observed demand alone. Across a range of system sizes the Global-View heuristic stays within a few percent of the optimum while scaling to over $10^4$ nodes. The Aggregated-View heuristic sacrifices some solution quality, but adapts continuously to each invocation. Under client mobility, centralized policies suffer from stale snapshots and recurring latency spikes, while the Aggregated-View maintains low and stable client-observed latency. Across all experiments, data placement proves more influential than function scheduling in determining the outcome.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [Recursive Self-Evolving Agents via Held-Out Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28374)
*Michael Nguyen,Quoc Nguyen,Paul Vuong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RSEA（递归自进化智能体），通过三层自然语言状态（策略、技能、流程手册）和无退化的保留集选择机制实现安全自进化。在四个基准测试中，RSEA在ALFWorld上表现最佳（69.3%），但不同方法各有所长；无保护的上线文演化（如Dynamic Cheatsheet）存在高风险和性能崩塌问题；RSEA的严格保留集选择确保了递归自进化的单调安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体通过演化自然语言工件（如反思、工作流、提示优化）提升性能的方法通常只在单一基准上报告成功，缺乏公平对比。作者希望进行苹果对苹果的比较研究，揭示这些方法的真实表现差异，并解决无保护上下文演化导致的高方差和不安全性问题。

Method: 提出RSEA（Recursive Self-Evolving Agent），维护三层自然语言状态：命令式策略（imperative strategy）、可复用技能（reusable skills）和流程手册（procedural playbook）。每次迭代中，RSEA从自身轨迹重写所有三层，并使用严格的keep-better门控机制：仅在候选版本在不重叠的保留集上不出现性能退化时才提交更新。实验在四个基准（ALFWorld, GAIA, τ-bench, WebShop）上对比六个基线方法（ReAct, Reflexion, GEPA, AWM, ACE, Dynamic Cheatsheet），所有方法使用同一本地骨干模型。

Result: 第一，没有通用的最优方法。RSEA在ALFWorld上是最强的单次方法（69.3% vs ReAct的64.6%，p=0.015），重试后达79.4%为最佳。但AWM在强骨干工具使用任务上表现最好。第二，无保护的上下文演化（Dynamic Cheatsheet）高方差且不安全：在ALFWorld上接近最优（70.7%），但在WebShop上崩塌（0.14 vs ReAct的0.43）。第三，RSEA的严格保留集选择使其单调安全：在所有基准上从未显著低于基础智能体，在演化上下文有害时退回vanilla ReAct。

Conclusion: 不同自然语言工件在不同任务上各有所长，不存在万能的解决方案；无保护的上下文演化存在高风险，需要严格的性能退化门控机制来确保安全性；RSEA的保留集选择策略有效平衡了自进化能力与性能稳定性。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly improved without weight updates by evolving a natural-language artifact, such as reflections, workflows, playbooks, cheatsheets, or optimized prompts, that conditions a frozen policy. Such methods are typically reported as wins on the single benchmark where they help. We study them apples-to-apples and surface a sharper picture. We introduce RSEA, a Recursive Self-Evolving Agent that carries a compact three-layer natural-language state: an imperative strategy, reusable skills, and a procedural playbook. Across generations, RSEA rewrites all three layers from its own trajectories and commits a candidate only if it does not regress on a disjoint held-out split, using a strict keep-better gate.
  Across four diverse benchmarks, ALFWorld, GAIA, (τ)-bench, and WebShop, and six faithful baselines, ReAct, Reflexion, GEPA, AWM, ACE, and Dynamic Cheatsheet, all evaluated on one shared local backbone, we find three main results. First, no artifact universally wins. RSEA is the strongest single-pass method on ALFWorld, reaching 69.3% compared with 64.6% for ReAct (McNemar (p=0.015)), and reaches 79.4% with retry, the best overall result. However, concrete-workflow induction, represented by AWM, is best on the strong-backbone tool-use tasks. Second, unguarded context evolution is high-variance and unsafe. Dynamic Cheatsheet, which curates context online without a held-out gate, is near-best on ALFWorld at 70.7%, yet collapses on WebShop, with a score of 0.14 compared with 0.43 for ReAct. Third, RSEA's strict held-out selection is what makes recursive self-evolution monotone-safe: it never significantly underperforms the base agent on any benchmark and falls back to vanilla ReAct when evolved context would hurt.

</details>


### [137] [Data and Evaluation Closed-Loop for Model Capability Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28471)
*Zhixuan Li,Jiangan Yuan,Han Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"能力切片"（capability slice）概念，构建了一个从评估故障到数据干预的闭环推理框架，并通过两个案例验证其有效性：一个案例排除了数据问题（定位到<EOS>掩码损失），另一个案例确认了数据问题（通过针对性采样提升数学推理能力）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型预训练中，模型能力是核心变量但无法直接观测。评估只能事后给出一个含噪的综合分数，而优化需要从失败反推数据修复，二者使用不兼容的语汇（基准名/样本正确性 vs. 数据源/领域/质量标签）。当前这种推理依赖直觉而非方法，缺乏系统性和可验证性。

Method: 提出"能力切片"（capability slice）：一组评估样本，共享背景条件、任务类型、求解操作和输出约束，既足够精确以定位单一弱点，又足够稳定以支持聚合。围绕此单元构建评估分类法、非指令数据分类法和映射规则，形成闭环：将基准级别的失败转化为有针对性、可测试的数据干预。

Result: 两个案例：（1）持续预训练导致BBH下降46.82%，闭环诊断发现是单个<EOS>掩码损失导致，而非推理能力减弱；修复后BBH恢复至66.44，超过原始检查点。（2）数学推理弱点通过求解操作分解为特定失败组合，针对性采样使AIME2025/AIME2026 Pass@128从6.67/0.00提升至26.67。同一闭环在两个方向相反的案例中均得出正确结论。

Conclusion: 评估到数据的推理可以成为常规化、可审计、可实验验证的流程，而非依赖直觉。能力切片及闭环框架为LLM预训练中的能力诊断和数据干预提供了系统方法论。

Abstract: Model capability is the central variable in LLM pre-training, yet is never observed directly: data shapes it prospectively, while evaluation reveals it only retrospectively, compressing samples, prompts, decoding, and scoring rules into one noisy score. Practical optimization runs this backward: a failure is observed first, and the engineer must infer the corpus fix. The two sides speak incompatible vocabularies -- benchmark names and per-sample correctness versus data sources, domains, and quality labels -- so this inference is usually intuition, not method. We close this gap with the \emph{capability slice}: a group of evaluation samples sharing background condition, task type, solving operation, and output constraint -- precise enough to localize a single weakness yet stable enough to survive aggregation, unlike a benchmark name, too coarse, or a single sample, too noisy. Built around this unit, an evaluation taxonomy, a non-instruction data taxonomy, and mapping rules form a closed loop turning a benchmark-level failure into a targeted, testable data intervention. We test this loop on two case studies pulling in opposite directions. First, the loop rules the data out: continued pre-training drives BBH down by $-46.82\%$, but diagnosis traces this to a single masked \texttt{\textless EOS\textgreater} loss rather than weakened reasoning; restoring it recovers BBH to $66.44$, above the original checkpoint, without changing the data. Second, the loop rules the data in: a persistent math-reasoning weakness is decomposed by solving operation into specific failing combinations, and a weakness-targeted sampling procedure built from it lifts AIME2025/AIME2026 Pass@128 from $6.67$/$0.00$ to $26.67$ each. The same unmodified loop reaches opposite, correct verdicts in both cases, showing the evaluation-to-data inference can be routine, auditable, and experimentally validated rather than intuitive.

</details>


### [138] [GPTNT: Benchmarking Real-Time Collaboration Between Multimodal Agents on Keep Talking And Nobody Explodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28514)
*Amit Parekh,Sabrina McCallum,Kareem Al-Hasan,Malvina Nikandrou,Alessandro Suglia,Ioannis Konstas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出GPTNT，一个基于合作视频游戏《Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes》的基准测试，要求两个智能体在实时、异步条件下通过有限通信协作拆解炸弹。实验表明当前最先进的多模态模型均无法在实时条件下成功拆解任何炸弹，而人类可以，揭示了模型在状态追踪、时间压力下的高效行动、歧义处理和错误恢复等方面的关键缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试孤立研究多模态模型在时间压力、信息不对称和不完美通信等协作条件，缺乏综合评估这些条件同时存在时模型协作能力的基准。

Method: 基于合作游戏Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes构建GPTNT基准：一个智能体能看见并操作炸弹但无拆解说明，另一个智能体有说明但无法看见或操作炸弹，两者需实时、异步通信协作。通过扣留说明手册、伙伴或两者来隔离模型从记忆中提取的知识与即时的协作推理。

Result: 所有测试的闭源和开源模型均未能在实时条件下成功拆解任何炸弹，而人类玩家可以做到。通过受控实验发现模型在状态追踪、时间压力下的高效行动、歧义处理和错误恢复方面存在关键缺陷。

Conclusion: GPTNT揭示出现有评估未覆盖的协作性能维度，当前最先进模型在真实协作场景中存在显著不足。由于基于真实游戏运行，GPTNT可利用程序生成和活跃的模组社区持续演化，避免被一次性解决。

Abstract: Multimodal models are increasingly deployed to solve tasks collaboratively with humans or other artificial agents. Existing benchmarks show that these models possess many of the required component capabilities, but the conditions that coincide in collaboration, including time pressure, information asymmetry, and imperfect communication, are usually studied in isolation. We introduce GPTNT, a benchmark built on the cooperative video game Keep Talking and Nobody Explodes, in which two agents must coordinate to defuse procedurally generated bomb puzzles against a live countdown. One agent can see and manipulate the bomb but does not have the defusal instructions; the other has the instructions but cannot see or manipulate the bomb. Neither agent can succeed alone: success requires effective and efficient communication. Unlike turn-based proxies, GPTNT requires agents to act asynchronously and communicate in real time. GPTNT is designed to separate collaboration from reliance on memorized solutions: the instruction manual, the partner, or both can be withheld to isolate what a model derives in the moment from what it already knows. We show that GPTNT poses a substantial challenge for state-of-the-art systems: none of the closed- or open-source models we test defuses a single bomb in real time, a bar that human players clear. Through controlled experiments, we identify critical weaknesses in state tracking, efficient action under time pressure, ambiguity handling, and error recovery. We release GPTNT as a benchmark for collaborative performance that current evaluations leave unmeasured. Because it runs on the real game, GPTNT benefits from procedural generation and inherits a living modding community, allowing the benchmark to evolve as models improve rather than being solved once and retired.

</details>


### [139] [IMCBench: A benchmark for multimodal LLMs in Image-grounded Medical Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28556)
*Maria Xenochristou,Ashutosh Joshi,Korosh Vatanparvar,Mohammad Abuzar Hashemi,Prasad Kasu,Deepak Bansal,Anchal Nema,Nivedita Wadhwa,Prashams S Jain,Rebecca Abraham,Will Kimbrough,Dilek Hakkani-Tur,Wilko Schulz-Mahlendorf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了IMCBench，一个基于图像的多轮医疗对话基准，用于评估多模态大模型在安全性、准确性和不确定性表达三个临床维度上的表现。实验表明，Claude Opus 4.6整体最优（3.61分），但没有模型在所有维度上占优，且安全性在恶性和罕见病例中显著下降。消融实验显示，视觉输入和EHR上下文对安全引导均有贡献，准确描述不保证安全引导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗AI基准存在碎片化问题：有的支持多轮对话但缺少图像，有的提供多模态输入但仅聚焦单轮问答。缺乏一个同时包含图像和多轮对话的综合评估基准，无法真实模拟临床场景中医生与患者的交互过程。

Method: 构建IMCBench基准：将真实的公开临床图像与合成的患者档案配对，生成模拟真实医患交互的多轮对话。采用LLM-as-Jury评分机制，经专家临床医生标注校准后，从安全性、准确性和不确定性表达三个维度对模型进行1-5分评估。对8个前沿多模态模型（Claude、GPT、Nova、Llama四大家族）进行基准测试，并开展消融实验。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6总得分最高（3.61），随后是Claude Sonnet 4.6（3.30）和GPT-5.2（3.29）。没有模型在所有维度上占优。恶性与罕见病例的安全性分别下降0.27。消融实验显示，去除视觉输入和EHR上下文分别导致安全性平均下降0.18和0.23，更强的模型能更有效地利用视觉特征。

Conclusion: 准确的临床描述并不保证安全的患者引导，这凸显了在医疗AI评估中采用多维度评估框架的必要性。同时，视觉输入和临床背景信息对于模型提供安全指导均具有重要作用。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models and vision-language models have enabled reasoning over multimodal data, offering opportunities for clinical applications such as decision support and triaging. However, existing medical AI benchmarks are fragmented: some support multi-turn dialogues but lack images, while others provide multimodal inputs but focus on single-turn QA tasks. To address this gap, we introduce IMCBench, an image-grounded, multi-turn medical conversation benchmark that pairs real, publicly available clinical images with synthetic patient profiles to simulate realistic patient-clinician interactions. Each conversation is evaluated across three clinical dimensions: safety, accuracy, and appropriate use of uncertainty in diagnosis. We benchmark eight multimodal frontier models across four model families (Claude, GPT, Nova, and Llama), scoring each on a 1-5 scale using LLM-as-Jury scoring calibrated against expert clinician annotations. Our results show that Claude Opus 4.6 achieves the highest overall score (3.61), followed by Claude Sonnet 4.6 (3.30) and GPT-5.2 (3.29), though no model dominates all dimensions and safety degrades for both malignant and rare conditions ($Δ$ = -0.27 each). Ablation studies further reveal that both visual input and EHR context contribute to safe guidance (safety drops of 0.18 and 0.23 on average when each is removed), with stronger models leveraging visual features more effectively. Together, these findings demonstrate that accurate clinical description does not guarantee safe patient guidance, motivating the need for multi-dimensional evaluation frameworks in medical AI.

</details>


### [140] [Search for Truth from Reasoning: A Dynamic Representation Editing Framework for Steering LLM Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28589)
*Tianlong Wang,Yuhang Wang,Weibin Liao,Xin Gao,Xinyu Ma,Yang Lin,Yasha Wang,Liantao Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DynaSteer框架，通过分析推理链中"真实"的几何结构，利用模式聚类和Fisher-LDA提取纯净的真实信号，并基于前瞻熵动态选择性地干预和回滚推理轨迹，从而在保持正确轨迹的同时引导LLM走向真实答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如CoT、"Wait"提示）虽然鼓励模型多思考，但未能有效引导模型走向真实答案；表示编辑（RepE）提供了内在控制能力，但尚未被应用于动态推理轨迹中。

Method: DynaSteer框架包含三个关键技术：(1) 模式聚类，用于解耦推理流形；(2) Fisher-LDA，用于投影提纯后的真实信号；(3) 动态前瞻熵监控，选择性干预并回滚轨迹。

Result: 在MATH数学推理基准上验证了有效性，并在域外编程任务上进一步证实了泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过对推理链中"真实"几何结构的三项关键洞察（句子级编码、不确定性原理与衰减效应、噪声干扰），DynaSteer实现了对推理轨迹的精确引导，避免了对正确轨迹的附带损害。

Abstract: Current approaches to enhance Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning, such as Chain-of-Thought and "Wait" prompts, primarily encourage models to think more, yet often fail to guide them toward Truth. While Representation Editing (RepE) offers a intrinsic control, its application to dynamic reasoning trajectories remains underexplored. In this work, we bridge this gap by investigating the geometry of truth within unfolding reasoning chains. We uncover three critical insights: (1) Truth is encoded at the sentence level and is entangled with latent reasoning patterns; (2) Effective intervention follows an Uncertainty Principle and a Decay Effect, requiring localization to early, high-entropy forks; (3) Naive steering vectors suffer from noise, risking collateral damage to correct trajectories. Based on these findings, we propose DynaSteer, a dynamic RepE framework. DynaSteer employs pattern clustering to disentangle reasoning manifolds and utilizes Fisher-LDA to project purified truth. By dynamically monitoring lookahead entropy, it selectively steers and rolls back trajectories only when necessary. Comprehensive experimental results on several MATH benchmark verify the effectiveness of DynaSteer, and experiments on out-of-domain coding tasks further confirm its generalization ability. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/tianlwang/DynaSteer.

</details>


### [141] [Aristotelian Virtue Profiling of LLMs through Ethical Dilemmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28683)
*Ioannis Tzachristas,John Pavlopoulos*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出VirtueMap框架，基于亚里士多德美德伦理学，通过排序方法评估LLM在伦理困境中的美德表现，发现LLM在不同美德维度上存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在伦理决策中常面临多种可辩护但体现不同优先级的回应，现有框架难以系统描述这些模式，缺乏基于美德伦理学的评估方法。

Method: 构建7个非敏感伦理困境，每个困境设计5个回应；针对每个困境和美德定义回应排序（95%以上人类确认作为参照）；使用归一化Borda对齐计算排名分数，覆盖实践智慧、正义、诚实、勇气和节制五个美德维度。

Result: 在9个LLM家族上重复评估，平均排名一致性达90.3%，最大差异出现在勇气、节制和正义维度。

Conclusion: VirtueMap能有效刻画LLM的美德偏好模式，不同模型在特定美德维度上存在可量化的差异，并提供了交互式工具便于进一步分析。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) often face ethical tradeoffs in which several responses may be defensible but express different priorities, such as fairness, honesty, courage, or restraint. We introduce VirtueMap, a framework for describing these patterns through an Aristotelian virtue-ethics lens. Instead of asking for a single correct answer, VirtueMap asks humans or LLMs to rank all five responses to each of seven general, non-lethal, non-political, and non-religious ethical dilemmas. To define the reference orderings used for scoring, we first proposed, for each dilemma and virtue, an ordering of the five responses from most to least expressive of that virtue. We then collected more than 100 respondent evaluations per ordering and retained it as operational ground truth only when at least 95% confirmed it. Rankings are scored against these retained orderings using normalized Borda alignment, yielding profiles over Practical Wisdom, Justice, Truthfulness, Courage, and Temperance. We apply VirtueMap to nine LLM families in a repeated-run evaluation and find high mean rank consistency (90.3%), with the largest differences appearing on Courage, Temperance, and Justice. We also release an interactive website that computes profiles locally in the browser and compares respondents with measured LLM profiles.

</details>


### [142] [An AI agent for treatment reasoning over a biomedical tool universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28692)
*Shanghua Gao,Ayush Noori,Richard Zhu,Curtis Ginder,Zhenglun Kong,Xiaorui Su,Justin Kauffman,Benjamin S. Glicksberg,Joshua Lampert,Ankit Sakhuja,Ashwin Sawant,ATHENA-R1 Evaluation Consortium,David A. Clifton,Noa Dagan,Ran Balicer,Marinka Zitnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍ATHENA-R1，一个基于强化学习训练的AI治疗推理智能体，覆盖1939年以来所有FDA批准药物。通过两级自学习框架（多智能体系统构建工具/任务/推理轨迹进行监督微调，再以科学反馈进行强化学习），在3,168个药物推理任务和456个患者治疗案例中，开放药物推理准确率94.7%，治疗推理准确率82.9%，分别超过GPT-5达17.8和10.7个百分点。专家和医生的盲评均倾向该模型，其生成的药物不良事件假设在540万患者电子病历中得到验证（调整后优势比1.48-1.84）。研究表明治疗推理可重构为强化学习可训练的迭代证据收集过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 治疗推理本质上是一个迭代过程：需要在疾病背景、合并症、药物、禁忌症和不断演进的生物医学知识等多重约束下权衡候选方案，并随新证据的出现而修正，同时要基于可验证来源。这对AI长期构成挑战，因为AI需要在得出结论之前知道需要寻求什么证据。本文旨在将治疗推理重新定义为强化学习可训练的迭代证据收集过程。

Method: 提出ATHENA-R1，一个基于强化学习训练的AI治疗推理智能体。方法包含：(1) 覆盖1939年以来所有FDA批准药物及212个生物医学工具；(2) 两级自学习框架：第一步，多智能体系统构建工具、任务和推理轨迹用于监督微调；第二步，使用带有科学反馈的强化学习，奖励证据收集、基于工具的使用和逻辑非冗余性等推理质量指标。

Result: 在5个基准测试、3,168个药物推理任务和456个患者治疗案例上，ATHENA-R1开放药物推理准确率达94.7%，治疗推理准确率达82.9%，分别超过GPT-5达17.8和10.7个百分点。在28个罕见病组织的专家盲评中，所有标准均优于参考模型。在540万患者电子病历中验证的不良事件假设，调整后优势比为1.48-1.84，负对照无升高。

Conclusion: 治疗推理长期被认为对AI困难，因为它需要在得出结论前知道需要寻求什么证据。本文证明治疗推理可以被重构为一个迭代证据收集的可学习过程，强化学习可以有效训练AI执行这一过程。

Abstract: Treatment reasoning underpins every therapeutic decision, integrating disease context, comorbidities, medications, contraindications, and evolving biomedical knowledge to select an appropriate therapy. It is inherently iterative: candidates are weighed against many constraints, revised as evidence emerges, and grounded in verifiable sources. Here we introduce ATHENA-R1, an AI agent for treatment reasoning across all FDA approved drugs since 1939, trained by reinforcement learning over a universe of 212 biomedical tools. At each step it identifies missing information, selects and runs relevant tools, and incorporates the evidence. To train it without human-annotated traces, we build a two-level self-learning framework: multi-agent systems construct the tools, tasks, and reasoning trajectories for supervised fine-tuning, then reinforcement learning with scientific feedback rewards reasoning quality (evidence gathering, grounded tool use, logical non-redundancy). Across five benchmarks of 3,168 drug reasoning tasks and 456 patient treatment cases, ATHENA-R1 outperforms language models and tool-use systems, reaching 94.7% accuracy on open-ended drug reasoning and 82.9% on treatment reasoning, 17.8 and 10.7 points above GPT-5. In blinded evaluations by experts from 28 rare disease organizations, it is preferred over reference models on all criteria, and physicians rated it favorably on complex hospitalized cardiovascular and infectious-disease cases. Adverse-event hypotheses it generated, tested in electronic health records from 5.4 million patients, reached adjusted odds ratios of 1.48-1.84, with no elevation among negative controls. Because it requires knowing what evidence to seek before concluding, treatment reasoning has long been hard for AI; we show it can be reframed as a learnable process of iterative evidence gathering that reinforcement learning can train AI to perform.

</details>


### [143] [COMPASS: Grounding Composition-Intent Guidance in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28696)
*Ziqi Zhou,Weize Quan,Mining Tan,Zhihan Chen,Dandan Zheng,Jingdong Chen,Jun Zhou,Weiming Dong,Dong-Ming Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COMPASS是首个统一的多模态框架，通过共享专家令牌τ_c将构图感知与可控生成集成于单一系统，在MoE骨干网络中注入构图专业知识，并利用大规模数据集Comp-11实现显著的构图理解与生成性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型在细粒度构图识别上不可靠，且难以将构图意图转化为可控生成，缺乏统一的构图意图锚定机制。

Method: 提出COMPASS框架，采用共享专家令牌τ_c作为核心意图锚点；感知侧通过MoE骨干网络以最小侵入方式注入构图专业知识并将推断意图蒸馏到τ_c；生成侧复用τ_c作为全局条件信号控制去噪轨迹；同时构建包含11类分类体系和推理增强标注的大规模数据集Comp-11。

Result: COMPASS在类别级构图理解上显著提升，并且相比强基线方法能够生成构图更一致、与提示更匹配的图像。

Conclusion: 共享意图锚点τ_c可有效桥接构图感知与生成，统一框架在双任务上均优于现有方法，为构图可控的多模态系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Composition is a high-level visual intent that governs where subjects are placed and how a scene is organized, yet current unified multimodal models remain unreliable at fine-grained composition recognition and struggle to turn such intent into controllable generation. We present COMPASS, the first unified multimodal framework that grounds composition-intent control in a single system spanning both composition perception and composition-guided generation, with a shared expert token $τ_c$ as the central intent anchor. On the perception side, COMPASS injects composition expertise into an MoE backbone in a minimally invasive manner and distills the inferred intent into $τ_c$. On the generation side, COMPASS reuses $τ_c$ as a global conditioning signal that steers the denoising trajectory, effectively converting passive composition analysis into explicit layout control. To support systematic instruction-following composition learning and evaluation at scale, we construct Comp-11, a large-scale dataset with an 11-class taxonomy and reasoning-augmented annotations. Extensive experiments show that COMPASS substantially improves category-level composition understanding and delivers more composition-consistent, prompt-faithful generation than strong baselines.

</details>


### [144] [BV-Blend: Uncertainty-Weighted Historical Baselines for Stable Critic-Free RL with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28707)
*Yupeng Chang,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出BV-Blend，一种无评论家的强化学习框架，通过结合提示局部统计和语义聚类历史统计来稳定优势估计，解决GRPO在零方差奖励场景下的训练停滞问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO等无评论家RLVR方法依赖组内奖励统计进行优势估计，当同一提示组内所有rollout获得相同奖励时，组内奖励方差为零，导致优势为零，阻碍冷启动学习，尤其在使用二元验证器时问题突出。

Method: BV-Blend维护每个语义聚类的EMA追踪奖励矩，通过标准误代理计算置信权重，将历史统计与提示局部统计混合得到标准化优势，用于PPO风格的裁剪更新。

Result: 在可验证推理基准上，BV-Blend相比组归一化方法提升了训练稳定性和性能，在组归一化方法可能停滞的设定下仍保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: BV-Blend有效解决了无评论家RLVR中因零奖励方差导致的训练停滞问题，为冷启动场景下的优势估计提供了更稳定的替代方案。

Abstract: Critic-free reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), exemplified by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), avoids training a value function (critic) and reduces memory and compute overhead relative to critic-based PPO pipelines for aligning large language models. However, GRPO-style advantage estimation depends on prompt-local (within-prompt-group) reward statistics and can be unstable. In particular, when all rollouts in a prompt group receive identical rewards, the within-group reward variance becomes zero, and group normalization yields zero advantages for that group, impeding learning in cold-start regimes with binary verifiers. We introduce BV-Blend, a critic-free framework that stabilizes advantage estimation by combining prompt-local on-policy statistics with semantic-cluster-conditioned historical moments.
