Through our analysis, we find enterprise data markedly differ from tabular benchmarks and we demonstrate that a tabular model that performs well on typical tabular benchmarks may perform poorly on real world enterprise data -- and vice versa. This lack of generalization underlines the need for additional benchmarks with enterprise-grade characteristics.

</details>


### [430] [Curvature-Weighted Gradient Diversity: A Noise Measure for Geometry-Adaptive SGD Schedules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30455)
*Muhammad Hamza,Ayush Goel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出曲率加权梯度多样性（CWGD），一种利用Hessian逆平方根加权梯度噪声的几何感知度量，并基于此设计CWGD-Cosine余弦学习率调度，在强凸二次目标下将渐近优化误差降低约20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准小批量SGD收敛分析使用单一方差项建模梯度噪声，忽略高曲率方向噪声影响较小（因学习率已受限）的事实，导致对有效优化噪声的估计不够紧致。

Method: 提出CWGD度量，用Hessian逆平方根加权每样本梯度多样性；基于CWGD设计余弦学习率调度（CWGD-Cosine）；使用基于Hutchinson的对角Hessian估计器（对二次目标精确）。

Result: 在强凸二次目标下，CWGD-Cosine比标准余弦退火降低渐近优化误差最多达2倍，并在不同条件数、批大小和噪声结构下一致实现约20%更低的最终优化误差，且二次设置中开销可忽略。

Conclusion: CWGD为一种有原则的几何感知优化噪声度量，为更一般学习问题的扩展奠定了基础；文中同时识别并修正了退化曲率估计器，分析了估计器鲁棒性，讨论了非凸优化中Hessian陈旧性等局限性。

Abstract: The standard convergence analysis of mini-batch stochastic gradient descent (SGD) models gradient noise using a single variance term that treats all parameter directions equally, ignoring the fact that noise in high-curvature directions has less impact because learning rates are already constrained there. We introduce Curvature-Weighted Gradient Diversity (CWGD), a geometry-aware measure that weights per-sample gradient diversity by the inverse square root of the Hessian, providing a tighter proxy for the effective optimization noise. For strongly convex quadratic objectives with diagonal Hessians and isotropic noise, we prove that a CWGD-modulated cosine learning-rate schedule can reduce the asymptotic optimization error floor by up to a factor of two compared with standard cosine annealing. We implement this idea as CWGD-Cosine using a Hutchinson-based diagonal Hessian estimator that is exact for quadratic objectives. Across a range of condition numbers, batch sizes, and noise structures, CWGD-Cosine consistently achieves approximately 20% lower final optimization error than standard cosine annealing while incurring negligible overhead in the quadratic setting. We also identify and correct a degenerate curvature estimator, analyze the robustness of the proposed estimator, and explicitly discuss the limitations of the method, including Hessian staleness in non-convex optimization. These results establish CWGD as a principled geometry-aware measure of optimization noise and motivate future extensions to more general learning problems.

</details>


### [431] [MuonSSM: Orthogonalizing State Space Models for Sequence Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30461)
*Thai-Khanh Nguyen,Ngoc-Bich-Uyen Vo,Thieu N. Vo,Tan M. Nguyen,Cuong Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MuonSSM通过动量通路和低秩牛顿-舒尔茨变换对记忆更新的几何结构进行显式条件化，在不改变循环转移矩阵的前提下稳定SSM训练，在语言、视觉和时间序列基准上显著提升长序列建模的准确性、鲁棒性和长上下文性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有状态空间模型（SSM）在长序列建模中常因一阶更新病态条件和更新几何不平衡而导致不稳定和记忆退化问题。

Method: 提出MuonSSM框架，引入动量增强通路和轻量级牛顿-舒尔茨变换作用于低秩输入注入，实现有界且谱条件良好的更新，同时保持并行扫描的计算复杂度。

Result: 在语言、视觉和时间序列多个基准上的实验表明，集成到不同SSM骨干中后，在准确性、鲁棒性和长上下文性能上均取得一致提升。

Conclusion: 更新几何的条件化是迈向稳定、可扩展序列建模的一条有原则的路径。

Abstract: State space models (SSMs) have emerged as efficient linear-time alternatives to attention for long-sequence modeling. However, existing SSMs often suffer from instability and memory degradation over extended horizons due to poorly conditioned first-order updates and unbalanced update geometry. We introduce MuonSSM, a general framework that stabilizes SSM training by explicitly conditioning the geometry of memory updates rather than the recurrent transition matrix. MuonSSM augments SSMs with a momentum-based pathway and a lightweight Newton Schulz transformation on low-rank input injections, yielding bounded and spectrally conditioned updates while preserving parallel scan complexity. Theory shows that MuonSSM improves gradient propagation, mitigates spectral amplification, and enriches memory representations over long horizons. Extensive experiments across language, vision, and time-series benchmarks show consistent gains in accuracy, robustness, and long-context performance when integrated into diverse SSM backbones. These results establish geometric conditioning of updates as a principled pathway to stable, scalable sequence modeling.

</details>


### [432] [Discovering Collaboration from Novelty: Random Network Distillation for Clustered Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30499)
*Davide Domini,Gianluca Aguzzi,Ivana Dusparic,Danilo Pianini,Mirko Viroli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于随机网络蒸馏的轻量级聚类联邦学习方法，通过客户端本地训练RND预测器并利用预测误差作为新颖性信号来估计客户端间相似性，在联邦训练前发现有意义的客户端分组，将聚类与学习解耦，适用于非独立同分布数据下的自主大规模分布式系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在非独立同分布数据下性能退化，单一全局模型难以代表客户端分布的多样性；现有聚类联邦学习方法将聚类分配与主训练循环耦合，增加了计算和通信开销。

Method: 每个客户端在本地数据上训练紧凑的随机网络蒸馏预测器，将其预测误差作为新颖性信号来估计与其他客户端的相似度，从而在联邦训练前发现有意义的客户端分组，无需共享原始数据或反复评估主模型。

Result: 聚类结果在运行时从局部新颖性估计中自发涌现，无需预先指定聚类数量或协作结构；该方法将聚类与学习解耦，提供了一种任务无关且高效的机制。

Conclusion: 所提方法适用于自主大规模分布式系统，能够在非独立同分布数据下实现自主协作，具有任务无关性和高效性。

Abstract: Federated Learning often suffers under non-independently and identically distributed data, where a single global model may fail to represent the diversity of client distributions. Clustered Federated Learning mitigates this issue by training specialized models for groups of similar clients, but existing approaches often couple cluster assignment with the main training loop, increasing computational and communication costs. We propose a lightweight clustering approach based on Random Network Distillation. Each client trains a compact Random Network Distillation predictor on its local data and uses its prediction error as a novelty signal to estimate similarity with other clients. This enables the discovery of meaningful client groups before federated training, without sharing raw data or repeatedly evaluating the main model. Crucially, the resulting federations emerge from local novelty estimates at runtime, making the method suitable for autonomous large-scale distributed systems where neither the number of clusters nor the collaboration structure can be specified a priori. Overall, by decoupling clustering from learning, the method provides a task-agnostic and efficient mechanism for autonomous collaboration under non-independently and identically distributed data.

</details>


### [433] [Muon learns balanced solutions in matrix factorization without slow saddle-to-saddle dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30509)
*Mark Rhee,Jamie Simon,Dhruva Karkada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon优化器在矩阵分解问题中与梯度下降的三点核心差异：避免慢速鞍点动力学、学习率可超越局部锐度阈值、以及守恒量的改变。同时揭示了权重对齐现象并设计了高效学习率调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Muon优化器在非线性参数动力学和表示学习中的行为差异，揭示其与梯度下降不同的动力学特性，为设计更高效的优化算法提供理论基础。

Method: 理论分析矩阵分解问题中Muon与梯度下降的动力学差异，通过守恒量分析、对齐速率推导和实验验证，并利用Muon的结构性质构造两步优化即可实现近完美对齐的学习率调度。

Result: 发现Muon三个主要动力学差异：1) 避免小初始化下的慢速鞍点间动力学，同步学习所有主模式；2) 学习率可超过局部损失锐度阈值，不受条件数限制；3) 守恒量从P^TP - Q^TQ变为√(P^TP)-√(Q^TQ)。两类优化器在零初始化下均能得到平衡解。

Conclusion: Muon优化器在矩阵分解中具有独特的动力学优势，可实现更快收敛和更稳定的训练，其对齐特性可用于构造高效学习率调度策略。

Abstract: Matrix factorization (i.e., problems of the form $\min_{\mathbf{P},\mathbf{Q}} \|\mathbf{M}^\star - \mathbf{P}^\top\mathbf{Q}\|_\mathrm{F}^2$) is a minimal learning problem that exhibits both nonlinear parameter dynamics and representation learning. In this setting, we study how parameter trajectories under the Muon optimizer differ from those of gradient descent. We identify three main dynamical differences: 1) Muon avoids the slow saddle-to-saddle dynamics from small initialization. Muon instead learns all the top modes of $\mathbf{M}^\star$ at the same rate, with the smaller modes converging first. 2) Muon remains stable even when the learning rate exceeds the critical threshold set by the local loss sharpness. This frees the learning rate from the condition number of the problem, enabling rapid convergence via exponential learning rate annealing. 3) Once the weights are aligned with each other and the target, Muon flow conserves the matrix quantity $\sqrt{\mathbf{P}^\top \mathbf{P}}-\sqrt{\mathbf{Q}^\top \mathbf{Q}}$, while gradient flow is known to conserve the matrix $\mathbf{P}^\top\mathbf{P} - \mathbf{Q}^\top\mathbf{Q}$. Despite having distinct conserved quantities, both optimizers find the so-called \textit{balanced} solution from vanishing initialization. When training from small random initialization, the weights spontaneously align early in training. We derive the alignment rates in simple settings and show that they predict the empirical alignment rates in general. Finally, we exploit structural properties of Muon to construct a learning rate schedule that achieves near-perfect alignment in only two optimization steps.

</details>


### [434] [Informational Frustration in Neural Manifolds: Shannon Bottlenecks and the Limits of Learnability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30512)
*Srinivasa Rao P.,Vangmayi P Reddy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个融合信息论、拓扑学和统计力学的统一框架，通过熵可学习性视界（ELH）和香农-拓扑瓶颈定理解释了深度网络的泛化能力，并将“顿悟”（grokking）现象解释为熵释放，最后提出了熵梯度下降（EGD）算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典理论（VC维、Rademacher复杂度）预测深度网络会过拟合，但现实中深度网络泛化能力很强，理论与现实之间存在巨大鸿沟。本文旨在弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出熵可学习性视界（ELH），建立香农-拓扑瓶颈定理，将数据流形的香农熵、决策边界的拓扑熵与权重空间的冯·诺依曼熵联系起来，分析泛化能力的硬极限。

Result: 证明了当目标边界的几何复杂度超过信息视界时，系统会发生突变的熵相变，陷入信息阻挫（Informational Frustration）的玻璃态记忆化阶段；将grokking解释为熵释放（Entropic Release）；提出熵梯度下降（EGD）算法。

Conclusion: 熵不仅是追踪不确定性的工具，更是决定机器能否学习的根本物理量。

Abstract: Why overparameterised deep networks generalise so remarkably well remains one of the most stubborn open questions in machine learning theory. Classical frameworks like VC dimension and Rademacher complexity predict catastrophic overfitting in modern models, leaving a massive theoretical gap between theory and reality. In this paper, we bridge this divide by introducing a unified framework that links information theory, topology, and statistical mechanics to map the hard limits of deep learning. Central to our approach is the Entropic Learnability Horizon (ELH): a fundamental law stating that a network can only truly learn a target function if the Shannon entropy of the data manifold outpaces the topological entropy of the function's decision boundary, balanced by the von Neumann entropy of the network's weight space. We establish the Shannon-Topological Bottleneck Theorem, proving that when a target boundary's geometric complexity exceeds this informational horizon, the system undergoes a sudden entropic phase transition. It falls into a state of Informational Frustration - a glassy, rigid memorization phase where generalization becomes thermodynamically impossible. Using this lens, we show that the enigmatic phenomenon of "grokking" is actually an Entropic Release, where weights abruptly reorganise to unlock the bottleneck. Finally, we translate this theory into practice with Entropic Gradient Descent (EGD), an optimization algorithm that dynamically manages weight entropy to keep learning on track. Ultimately, this work repositions entropy not just as a tool for tracking uncertainty but as the fundamental physical currency that dictates whether a machine can learn.

</details>


### [435] [ITSPACE: Monotone Gaussian Optimal Transport Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30523)
*Woojoo Na,Jennifer Dy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ITSPACE方法，通过近端优化直接优化Bures-Wasserstein目标，实现协方差矩阵的快速对齐，比现有方法更快达到低BW-gap解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协方差矩阵作为特征分布的紧凑描述子被广泛用于机器学习（如域适应、高斯嵌入），但现有优化方法（梯度下降、熵正则化）求解Bures-Wasserstein目标速度慢、计算开销大，需要更高效的轻量级内循环方法。

Method: 提出ITSPACE：一种近端MM（majorization-minimization）方法，在平方根分解中通过闭式更新直接优化精确BW目标。每次迭代满足BW目标的充分下降不等式；对于非精确极分解，给出显式证书间隙界控制偏差。天然保持PSD结构并支持秩受限因子。

Result: 在真实协方差对齐基准上，ITSPACE达到低BW-gap解的速度显著快于BW梯度下降、基于其他协方差几何的方法以及熵正则化样本OT基线。

Conclusion: ITSPACE作为轻量级内循环原语，适用于未标注目标批次下严格步骤和计算预算的域适应场景，兼顾收敛保证与计算效率。

Abstract: Covariance matrices serve as compact descriptors of feature distributions in many machine-learning pipelines, including domain adaptation and Gaussian embeddings. Under a centered Gaussian approximation, the unregularized Wasserstein-2 optimal-transport (OT) discrepancy admits a closed form on covariances given by the Bures-Wasserstein (BW) objective on the symmetric positive definite (SPD) cone. We propose ITSPACE (Iterative Transport for Stable Proximal Alignment of Covariance Embeddings), a proximal majorization-minimization method that directly optimizes this exact BW objective through closed-form updates in a square-root factorization. In exact arithmetic, each iteration satisfies a sufficient-decrease inequality for the BW objective; under inexact polar computations, we provide an explicit certificate-gap bound controlling deviations from exact descent. The resulting iterations preserve PSD structure by construction and naturally support rank-restricted factors, making ITSPACE well-suited as a lightweight inner-loop primitive in settings where adaptation must be performed from unlabeled target batches under strict step and compute budgets. Across real-world covariance-alignment benchmarks, ITSPACE reaches low-BW-gap solutions substantially faster than BW-gradient descent, methods based on other covariance geometries, and entropically regularized sample-OT baselines.

</details>


### [436] [Convergence of Continual Learning in Homogeneous Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30559)
*Matan Schliserman,Gon Buzaglo,Itay Evron,Daniel Soudry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文刻画了同质模型中的弱正则化持续分类问题，将其视为在任务间隔集上的顺序投影。该结果推广了先前仅限于静态（单任务）深度模型或持续线性模型的分析。研究表明全局收敛通常失败，即使在数据线性但参数非线性的简单模型中也如此。但利用非凸投影理论的结果，我们识别了同质深度网络的规律性，保证了随机和循环任务序列下的局部线性收敛。最后将分析扩展到持续回归，统一了同质模型的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将弱正则化持续分类问题统一为顺序投影框架，推广先前仅限于静态深度模型或持续线性模型的分析，并解决持续学习中全局收敛性的理论挑战。

Method: 通过将弱正则化持续分类在同质模型中刻画为在任务间隔集上的顺序投影；利用非凸投影理论分析同质深度网络的规律性，研究随机和循环任务序列下的局部线性收敛性；并将方法扩展到持续回归。

Result: 证明了全局收敛通常失败（即便在简单模型中）；但识别了同质深度网络的规律性，保证随机和循环任务序列下的局部线性收敛；并将持续回归纳入统一框架。

Conclusion: 本文为同质模型中的持续学习提供了统一的理论框架，揭示了全局收敛的局限性，同时指出了在特定条件下局部收敛的保证，为后续研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We characterize weakly regularized continual classification in homogeneous models as sequential projections onto task margin sets. This result generalizes prior analyses restricted to either stationary (single-task) deep models or continual linear models. We show that global convergence generally fails, even for simple models linear in data but nonlinear in parameters. Nevertheless, by leveraging results from nonconvex projection theory, we identify regularity properties of homogeneous deep networks that guarantee local linear convergence under random and cyclic task sequences. Finally, we extend our analysis to continual regression, unifying the framework for homogeneous models.

</details>


### [437] [SWE-INTERACT: Reimagining SWE Benchmarks as User-Driven Long-Horizon Coding Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30573)
*Mohit Raghavendra,Anisha Gunjal,Aakash Sabharwal,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SWE-Interact是一个用于评估编程智能体在多轮、交互式、用户驱动的软件工程任务中表现的新测试平台。与一次性提供完整需求的传统基准不同，它通过用户模拟器逐步揭示需求、提供反馈和修订，测试智能体发现用户意图、适应变化需求的能力。实验发现，强模型在单轮任务中表现好，但在多轮交互任务中性能显著下降（从约50%降至约25%），即使最强模型也存在过度自主编码、遗忘需求等技术问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前沿SWE基准通常提供完整的需求说明，评估智能体自主实现能力，但忽略了现实开发流程中用户需求逐步明确、交互迭代的核心特征，缺乏测试智能体在多轮交互中理解用户意图和适应变化需求的能力。

Method: 构建SWE-Interact测试平台，设计用户模拟器模拟真实开发者工作流：从模糊或不完整的指令开始，逐步揭示需求，检查智能体工作空间，提供针对性反馈和修订，直至任务目标完全传达。基于大规模真实编程智能体交互研究设计交互流程。

Result: 强模型在单轮任务中解决约50%的问题，但在SWE-Interact多轮任务中仅解决约25%。最强模型（如Opus 4.8和GPT 5.5）在模糊初始指令下表现良好，能坚持到需求完全揭示，但存在过度自主编码、遗忘需求和技术错误。弱模型在模糊性下表现差，容易过早放弃、忽略指令并重写代码。

Conclusion: SWE-Interact衡量了前沿模型开发中一个正交且现实的能力轴：与用户交互中的目标发现和迭代细化能力。强单轮SWE任务表现无法可靠迁移到多轮用户驱动的工作流中。

Abstract: We introduce SWE-Interact, a new testbed for evaluating coding agents on multi-turn, interactive, user-driven software engineering tasks. Existing frontier SWE benchmarks typically provide complete requirements upfront and evaluate agents on autonomous implementation. In contrast, SWE-Interact places agents in a realistic developer workflow: a carefully designed user simulator starts with vague or incomplete instructions, progressively reveals requirements, inspects the agent's workspace, and provides targeted feedback, revisions, and new constraints until the full task goal has been handed off. Grounded in large-scale studies of real coding-agent interactions, this setup tests whether agents can discover user intent, adapt to evolving requirements, and build on their own prior work. Across a suite of frontier and open-weight models, we find that strong performance on single-turn SWE tasks does not reliably transfer to multi-turn, user-driven workflows: the best-performing models solve roughly 50% of single-turn baseline tasks but only 25% of the corresponding SWE-Interact tasks. The strongest models in our evaluation, including Opus 4.8 and GPT 5.5, start strong even in the face of vague initial instructions, persevere until all the requirements are surfaced by the user, integrate them better and write clean code. However, they still suffer from over-agentic coding, forgetting requirements and technical mistakes. Weaker models start poorly under ambiguity, give up early, forget or ignore instructions and rework their code more. Overall, SWE-Interact measures an orthogonal, real-world capability axis for frontier model development: interactive goal discovery and iterative refinement with a user in the loop.

</details>


### [438] [The Fundamental Limits of Valid Transport Map Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30574)
*Sivaraman Balakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在极小极大框架下形式化了任意有效传输映射的估计任务，发现标准稳定性假设下估计任意传输映射与估计最优传输映射统计难度相同，但当稳定性假设不成立时，替代传输映射可被更准确地学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多现代生成模型（如扩散模型、归一化流、流匹配）估计的是传输映射或传输计划，而非明确针对最优传输（OT）映射。由于生成建模中传输成本本身并不重要，因此有理由追求在统计或计算上更易处理的映射。本文旨在为估计任意有效传输映射建立严格的极小极大统计框架，以理解传输式生成方法的统计极限。

Method: 采用极小极大（minimax）统计决策理论框架，形式化定义"任意有效传输映射"的估计问题，并在标准OT稳定性假设（如某种形式的Lipschitz或强凸性条件）下推导样本复杂度下界，同时通过反例说明当稳定性假设不成立时的统计优势。

Result: 在标准OT稳定性假设下，估计任意有效传输映射与估计OT映射的统计难度相同（样本复杂度下界一致）；当稳定性假设失效时，替代传输映射可以显著更准确地被学习。

Conclusion: 本文的极小极大框架为理解基于传输的现代生成方法的统计极限提供了严格基础，阐明了何时以次优传输映射为目标可以带来实际的统计优势。

Abstract: Many modern generative modeling methods, including diffusion models, normalizing flows, and flow matching, estimate transport maps or plans between distributions without explicitly targeting an optimal transport (OT) map. In applications like generative modeling, the transport cost itself is irrelevant, and this makes it natural to target maps which are more tractable from either a statistical or computational standpoint. In this short note, we formalize the task of estimating any valid transport map in a rigorous minimax framework. One consequence of this framing is that it yields sample complexity lower bounds for any method whose learned object is evaluated as a transport map or plan, including flow matching and diffusion-based generative models, in settings where direct analysis would be challenging due to the analytic complexity of the methods and their target maps. We observe that, under standard, though strong, stability assumptions from the OT literature, estimating any valid transport map is statistically as hard as estimating the OT map. We complement these results with some examples showing that when these stability assumptions fail, alternative transport maps can be learned substantially more accurately than the OT map. Our minimax framing provides a rigorous foundation for understanding the statistical limits of modern transport-based generative methods and clarifies when targeting sub-optimal maps can provide real statistical advantages.

</details>


### [439] [C$^{2}$R: Cross-sample Consistency Regularization Mitigates Feature Splitting and Absorption in Sparse Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30609)
*Haoran Jin,Xiting Wang,Shijie Ren,Hong Xie,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出跨样本一致性正则化（C²R）方法，通过惩罚方向相似潜伏单元的共激活，有效缓解稀疏自编码器中普遍存在的特征分裂和特征吸收问题，在提升潜伏单元可解释性的同时保持重建保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）在扩展字典规模时面临严重的特征分裂（将连贯概念碎片化为非原子潜伏单元）和特征吸收（在通用特征中产生任意例外）问题，这些问题源于跨样本缺乏约束导致的单一样本优化中概念的不一致分配，严重损害了潜伏单元的可信度。

Method: 提出跨样本一致性正则化（C²R），通过惩罚批次中方向相似潜伏单元的共激活，显式鼓励每个语义特征由统一的潜伏单元跨样本一致地表示。

Result: 综合评估表明C²R有效缓解了特征分裂和特征吸收问题，同时关键地保持了重建保真度，在不降低模型性能的前提下增强了潜伏单元的可解释性。

Conclusion: C²R提供了一种原则性解决方案，通过在SAE训练中引入跨样本一致性约束，从根本上解决了字典规模扩大带来的特征分裂与吸收问题，实现了可解释性与重建性能的双赢。

Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) are widely used to interpret large language models by decomposing activations into sparse, human-understandable features, but scaling to large dictionaries exposes fundamental challenges. Systematic studies reveal pervasive feature splitting that fragments coherent concepts into non-atomic latents and widespread feature absorption that creates arbitrary exceptions in general features, severely compromising latent reliability. These issues stem from inconsistent latent assignment across samples: without cross-sample constraints, per-sample optimization often allows a single underlying concept to be inconsistently distributed across multiple redundant or interfering latents. To address this, we introduce C$^2$R (\underline{\textbf{C}}ross-sample \underline{\textbf{C}}onsistency \underline{\textbf{R}}egularization). C$^2$R explicitly encourages that each semantic feature is consistently represented by a unified latent across the batch by penalizing the co-activation of directionally similar latents. Comprehensive evaluation demonstrates that C$^2$R effectively mitigates both splitting and absorption while, crucially, preserving reconstruction fidelity, providing a principled solution that enhances latent interpretability without degrading model performance. Source code is available at https://github.com/hr-jin/Cross-sample-Consistency-Regularization.

</details>


### [440] [Pessimism's Paradox: Conservative Offline Training Amplifies Reward Hacking During Online Adaptation in Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30627)
*Subramanyam Sahoo,Aman Chadha,Vinija Jain,Divya Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"保守离线训练能安全支撑后续在线适配"的常见假设。实验证明，在DPO框架下，更高的保守性（β越大）反而会单调增加奖励破解（reward hacking）的程度。机制分析揭示：高β压缩策略熵 → 输出多样性降低 → 虽有更近的表示距离，但集成模型的不确定性增加并被在线优化更快利用。提出了校准的最优保守性水平β*。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑并挑战"保守离线训练是安全在线适配基础"这一广泛持有的直觉，揭示高保守性反而加剧奖励破解风险的反直觉现象。

Method: 在Qwen3-14B策略上使用DPO训练三个保守性级别（β_lo, β_mid, β_hi），然后对各checkpoint进行在线适配（针对3×Qwen3-1.7B奖励集成模型），在GSM8K精确匹配准确率上测量真实性能。使用Goodhart Gap及其曲线下面积（AUGC）量化奖励破解程度；通过策略熵、响应多样性（余弦距离）和集成不确定性进行机制分析。

Result: 更高的离线保守性（β越大）单调增加奖励破解损害，Spearman ρ=1.0（三个条件）。机制上：高β压缩策略熵 → 响应多样性降低 → 尽管表示距离更近，但集成模型的不确定性增加并被在线优化更快利用。拟合出功率律曲线并识别出最优保守性水平β*。

Conclusion: 领域需要的是校准的（calibrated）而非最大化的（maximal）保守性。过度保守反而有害，应平衡对齐保真度与破解脆弱性。

Abstract: Conservative offline training is widely advocated as a safe foundation for subsequent online adaptation: if a policy stays close to well-supported behaviour, the argument goes, it is less likely to exploit imperfections in a learned reward model. We challenge this intuition empirically and mechanistically. We train a Qwen3-14B policy under Direct Preference Optimisation (DPO) with three levels of conservatism ($β\in \{β_{\mathrm{lo}}, β_{\mathrm{mid}}, β_{\mathrm{hi}}\}$ derived from empirical log-ratio percentiles), then adapt each checkpoint online against a learned reward ensemble (3\,$\times$\,Qwen3-1.7B) while measuring true performance on GSM8K exact-answer accuracy. We find that \emph{higher offline conservatism monotonically increases reward-hacking damage}, measured by the Goodhart gap and its area under the curve (AUGC), with Spearman $ρ= 1.0$ across all three conditions. Mechanistic analysis reveals a three-link causal chain: (i) high-$β$ DPO compresses policy entropy, (ii) Low-entropy policies generate responses with reduced diversity, concentrating in a narrow region of the reward model's training distribution (lower pairwise cosine distance), and (iii) despite this proximity, ensemble disagreement (epistemic uncertainty) increases with $β$ and is exploited faster during online optimisation. We further fit a power-law curve to the $(β, \augc)$ data and identify a practical optimal conservatism level $β^{\star}$ that balances alignment fidelity against hacking vulnerability. Our results suggest that the field needs \emph{calibrated}, not \emph{maximal}, conservatism.

</details>


### [441] [One-Step Gradient Delay is Not a Barrier for Large-Scale Asynchronous Pipeline Parallel LLM Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30634)
*Philip Zmushko,Egor Petrov,Nursultan Abdullaev,Mikhail Khrushchev,Samuel Horváth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了异步流水线并行中梯度陈旧性导致训练不稳定的普遍认知，证明在单步延迟下，优化器选择是关键因素：AdamW在该场景下表现不佳，而Muon等新方法具有鲁棒性。作者引入了一种与优化器无关的纠错机制，并在多达100亿参数的模型上验证了该方法能弥合与同步训练的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异步流水线并行（如PipeDream-2BW）能消除同步流水线中的气泡，最大化吞吐量，但因梯度陈旧性问题被认为训练不稳定，导致实际应用受限。本文试图挑战这一假设，探究在单步延迟下性能下降的真实原因。

Method: 1) 对AdamW和Muon等优化器在单步延迟下的表现进行全面的实证分析；2) 提出一种与优化器无关的、基于Error Feedback的纠错机制来缓解延迟效应；3) 提供Muon在有无该纠错机制下的收敛性理论分析。

Result: AdamW在单步延迟下性能严重下降，而Muon表现出强鲁棒性；所提出的纠错机制能进一步弥合与同步训练的性能差距；在多达100亿参数的模型上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 异步流水线并行在合适的优化器（如Muon）和纠错机制下具有实际可行性，可以兼顾高吞吐量和模型性能，为大规模LLM训练提供了有前途的方案。

Abstract: Modern large-scale LLM pretraining benefits from utilizing Pipeline Parallelism; however, synchronous implementations leave GPUs idle during pipeline bubbles, wasting computational resources. Asynchronous Pipeline Parallelism eliminates these bubbles, maximizing throughput at the cost of gradient staleness. Among asynchronous schedules, PipeDream-2BW is particularly appealing: unlike the original PipeDream schedule, it ensures a constant one-step gradient delay regardless of pipeline depth. However, its adoption remains limited due to the common belief that optimizing under staleness is fundamentally unstable. In this work, we challenge this assumption, demonstrating that degradation under one-step delay depends strongly on optimizer choice rather than being an intrinsic limitation. We provide the first comprehensive empirical analysis showing that while AdamW, the predominant optimizer at the time when PipeDream-2BW was introduced, indeed suffers from severe degradation, recent methods like Muon exhibit strong robustness under a one-step delay. We introduce an optimizer-agnostic Error Feedback-inspired correction to further mitigate delay effects. We provide supporting theoretical analysis demonstrating convergence for Muon with and without this correction. Extensive evaluation on models up to 10B parameters confirms that our strategies bridge the performance gap with synchronous training, highlighting the practical potential of asynchronous pipeline parallelism at scale.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [442] [Evidence-Driven LLM Agent for C-to-Synthesizable-C Conversion and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28409)
*Zhe Zhao,Hongbing Lang,Zhihan Xiao,Luke Ztz Hu,John Imoleayo Adebisi,Songping Mai*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多智能体协作的闭环工作流，用于将C程序自动转换为HLS可综合的C代码，通过四阶段验证器、渐进式不匹配定位链（PMLC）和基于检索增强生成（RAG）的修复卡池，显著优于现有SOTA模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HLS工具链仅支持C的子集（HLS-C），导致大量C程序无法完成编译、CSim、综合、CoSim四个阶段的流水线。现有基于LLM的HLS代码修复系统仅覆盖早期阶段，且直接输入原始工具日志，修复结果脆弱且难以复现。

Method: 1) 构建由多智能体协作的端到端闭环工作流，以四阶段验证器为闭环反馈；2) 提出渐进式不匹配定位链（PMLC），通过日志归一化、AST反向切片和双迹线插桩定位CSim/CoSim不匹配；3) 设计带类型查询的两阶段证据RAG，依托自进化、按家族路由的修复卡池。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的工作流在性能上显著优于所有可比较的现有最先进模型。

Conclusion: 通过将C到HLS-C的转换形式化为闭环的生成-验证-诊断-修复问题，并引入PMLC和证据RAG机制，有效提升了HLS代码转换的自动化程度和修复质量。

Abstract: Software-compilable C programs routinely fail to complete the four-stage pipeline of a high-level synthesis (HLS) toolchain -- compilation, C simulation (CSim), synthesis, and C/RTL co-simulation (CoSim) -- because HLS accepts only a synthesizable subset of C (HLS-C). Yet most existing large language model (LLM) systems built for HLS code repair only cover the early pipeline stages and feed raw tool logs directly to the model, yielding brittle and hard-to-reproduce fixes. We formulate C-to-HLS-C conversion as a closed-loop generation-verification-diagnosis-repair problem on an HLS tool (Xilinx Vitis), contributing three components: an end-to-end workflow of cooperating agents closed by the four-stage verifier under strict evidence isolation; a Progressive Mismatch Localization Chain (PMLC) that localizes CSim/CoSim mismatches through log normalization, AST backward slicing, and dual-trace instrumentation; and a typed-query, two-stage evidence RAG backed by a self-evolving, family-routed repair-card pool. Experimental results show that the proposed workflow substantially outperforms all comparable state-of-the-art models.

</details>


### [443] [SHIFT: Dynamic Compute Relocation Framework for Communication-Aware Chiplet-Based Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28754)
*Arvin Delavari,Leonid Popryho,Inna Partin-Vaisband,Boris Vaisband*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出SHIFT——一种面向大规模异构系统的拓扑无关架构，通过将计算节点上下文和数据整体迁移至更优位置（而非单纯移动数据）来优化通信。基于chiplet架构和多带宽域集成平台，采用改良最短路径算法与轻量级ML辅助策略进行自适应调度路由。仿真结果显示任务迁移成功率75.2%-97.9%，延迟改善16.4%-62.5%，吞吐量提升最高12.5倍，能效提升58.3%；在标准LLM工作负载上，运行时、吞吐量和能效分别提升4.9倍、5.9倍和1.8倍，超越现有晶圆级LLM服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模异构系统的通信复杂度日益增长，传统的片上网络仅进行数据迁移，不足以缓解通信瓶颈，亟需一种将计算上下文与数据整体迁移至更优位置的拓扑无关方法。

Method: 提出SHIFT拓扑无关架构：基于chiplet和多带宽域细间距集成平台，采用多层路由在功能chiplet与智能utility chiplet间进行路由和计算迁移；使用改良最短路径算法结合轻量级ML辅助策略推断流量条件以提高自适应性。

Result: 随机测试：迁移成功率75.2%-97.9%，延迟改善16.4%-62.5%（最高76.8%），吞吐量提升最高12.5倍，单位面积功耗降低~8%，能效提升最高58.3%，性能提升18%。LLM负载：运行时、吞吐量、能效分别提升4.9倍、5.9倍、1.8倍，超越现有晶圆级LLM服务。

Conclusion: SHIFT通过计算迁移而非仅数据迁移，在多种配置下显著提升了大规模异构系统的通信效率、延迟、吞吐量和能效，验证了与大规模平台和应用（包括LLM工作负载）的兼容性。

Abstract: The increasing communication complexity of large-scale heterogeneous systems has motivated runtime methodologies for communication-aware workload placement and routing optimization. These communication limitations are addressed in this paper by proposing SHIFT, a novel topology-agnostic approach that transfers compute node context and data to a more suitably positioned node, rather than only shifting data as in conventional networks-on-chip. The proposed strategy is evaluated on a chiplet-based architecture utilizing a fine-pitch integration platform featuring multiple bandwidth-domains for heterogeneous workloads. The proposed architecture employs multi-layered routing between functional or memory chiplets and utility chiplets, which serve as intelligent nodes for routing and compute relocation. Adaptive scheduling and routing utilize a modified shortest-path algorithm for large-scale systems, complemented by a lightweight ML-assisted policy that infers traffic conditions to improve adaptivity. To establish a performance baseline, the initial assessment uses random instruction vectors and data patterns to evaluate the fundamental capabilities of SHIFT. Simulation results exhibit successful relocations over total trials ranging from 75.2% to 97.9% across configurations, with average latency improvements of 16.4%-62.5% and a maximum of 76.8%. In addition, throughput is improved by up to 12.5x, power dissipation per unit area is reduced by ~8%, energy-per-bit is reduced by up to 58.3%, and performance is improved by 18%. To evaluate efficiency under high logic and data density, the framework was tested on standard LLM workloads. Results exhibit average improvements of 4.9x, 5.9x, and 1.8x in runtime, throughput, and energy-efficiency, respectively, surpassing state-of-the-art wafer-scale LLM services and demonstrating compatibility with large-scale platforms and applications.

</details>


### [444] [Harvesting AI Computation at the Edge via Generic Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29518)
*Yihan Wang,Huiru Yan,Luxin Zhang,Long Cheng,Weiwei Chen,Ying Wang,Lei Zhang,Cheng Liu,Huawei Li*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用通用近似技术收割闲置AI计算资源的框架，通过神经架构搜索将传统计算任务自动转换为神经网络模型，并在AI引擎空闲时部署，从而在不影响主要AI工作负载的情况下减轻通用处理器的负担，实验表明该方法在AIoT处理器上显著提升了边缘处理任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备中的AI芯片为满足峰值工作负载而设计，但常因时空冗余而利用率不足，造成计算资源浪费；同时通用处理器因资源受限难以处理信号处理等密集型计算任务。为平衡这一矛盾，本文旨在利用闲置的AI计算资源来加速通用计算任务。

Method: 提出一个计算资源收割框架：(1) 通过神经架构搜索(NAS)方法将传统计算任务自动转换为神经网络模型；(2) 设计运行时调度器，在AI芯片空闲期间将近似版本的通用计算任务卸载到AI引擎上执行，且不损害主要AI工作负载的性能。

Result: 在代表性AIoT处理器上的实验表明，所提出的AI计算收割策略在一系列边缘处理任务上实现了显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过将通用计算任务近似为神经网络模型并利用AI芯片的空闲算力执行，可以有效缓解边缘设备中AI芯片资源浪费与通用处理器算力不足之间的矛盾，为AIoT场景下的异构计算资源优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: With the widespread adoption of AI in various IoT scenarios such as smart sensing and processing, AI chips have become a common component at the edge. These chips are typically specialized for structured neural network (NN) processing and are designed to meet peak workload demands. However, they are often underutilized and suffer from considerable computational waste due to temporal or spatial redundancy in processing. Conversely, general-purpose processing engines at the edge may struggle with compute-intensive tasks such as signal processing and complex numerical operations because of stringent resource constraints. To address this imbalance, we propose a framework that harvests unused AI computation resources using general-purpose approximation techniques. The core idea is to automatically convert traditional computing tasks into neural network models via a representative neural architecture search (NAS) method. These approximate versions of general-purpose tasks are then deployed on AI engines during their idle periods. Specifically, we introduce a runtime scheduler that offloads these tasks to AI chips without compromising the performance of primary AI workloads, thereby alleviating the burden on general-purpose processors. Experiments on a representative AIoT processor show that our proposed AI computation harvesting strategy delivers substantial performance improvements across a set of edge processing tasks.

</details>


### [445] [HBM Is Not All You Need: Efficient Disaggregated LLM Serving across Memory-heterogeneous Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.29986)
*Zhixiang Wei,Yun Wang,James Yen,Mingyuan Xia,Zhengwei Qi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: HMA-Serve 提出了一种跨厂商、内存异构的 LLM 推理分离架构，将 GDDR 加速器用于计算密集的 prefill 阶段，HBM GPU 用于内存受限的 decode 阶段，通过逐阶段量化、计算-传输流水线和延迟反量化三项技术，在无需共享 KV 格式和软件栈的前提下，实现了最高 3.2 倍的有效吞吐量和 4.8 倍的每美元有效吞吐量提升，且不影响生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 推理系统将 prefill 和 decode 分离到同厂商的 HBM GPU 上，但 prefill 阶段 HBM 带宽利用率极低，导致成本浪费。若将更便宜的 GDDR 加速器用于 prefill、HBM GPU 用于 decode，虽能降低成本，但跨厂商带来 KV 格式不兼容和软件栈不共享两大障碍。

Method: (1) 逐阶段量化：prefill 使用厂商原生低精度格式以提升吞吐，decode 保持高精度 BF16；(2) 计算-传输流水线：将每层的 KV 缓存传输与后续层的 prefill 计算重叠，降低首 token 延迟；(3) 延迟反量化：传输原始量化字节，在 decode GPU 上按需重建，减少网络带宽和 HBM 使用。

Result: 在 Qwen3 4B-32B 四个模型和三种生产级 trace 上，HMA-Serve 相比最先进的内存同构方法实现最高 3.2 倍有效吞吐量提升，4.8 倍每美元有效吞吐量提升，且生成质量无可测量损失。

Conclusion: 跨厂商内存异构分离式 LLM 推理是可行的，且能在大幅降低成本的同时保持或提升性能，为未来数据中心 GPU 选型提供了新路径。

Abstract: LLM inference comprises a compute-bound prefill phase and a memory-bound decode phase, and recent systems disaggregate them onto separate hardware. Yet today's datacenter GPUs rely on costly HBM whose bandwidth sits almost entirely idle during prefill. LLM serving across memory-heterogeneous accelerators (MemHA) pairs GDDR-based accelerators for prefill with HBM-based GPUs for decode, promising lower cost without sacrificing performance. Pushed to its most economical form, MemHA serving is inherently cross-vendor, since the best-suited chip for each phase may come from a different vendor. This breaks two assumptions that single-vendor disaggregation takes for granted -- a KV format both ends consume natively, and a shared software stack. We present \textbf{HMA-Serve}, a MemHA-centric disaggregated serving system pairing GDDR-based accelerators for prefill with HBM-based GPUs for decode efficiently. HMA-Serve achieves this through (1) phase-wise quantization, applying vendor-native low precision for high-throughput prefill while keeping decode in high-precision BF16, (2) a compute-transfer pipeline that overlaps each layer's KV cache transfer with later-layer prefill to reduce time-to-first-token (TTFT), and (3) deferred dequantization, shipping raw quantized bytes and reconstructing them lazily on the decode GPU to reduce network bandwidth and HBM usage. Across four Qwen3 models (4B--32B) and three production traces, HMA-Serve delivers up to $3.2\times$ higher goodput than state-of-the-art memory-homogeneous methods and $4.8\times$ higher goodput-per-dollar, with no measurable loss on generation-quality benchmarks.

</details>


### [446] [Mega: A 22 nm Convolutional Spiking Neural Network Accelerator Achieving 0.375 pJ/SOP for Efficient Edge Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30039)
*Rick Luiken,Manil Dev Gomony,Sander Stuijk*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Mega的数字架构，用于卷积脉冲神经网络（SNN），通过高度并行的3×3卷积加速、统一数据存储器和高效脉冲映射处理，在22nm FDSOI工艺上实现了0.375 pJ/SOP的能效，较现有技术提升4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN加速器未能充分利用卷积层的固有并行性，且缺乏适应不同层间内存需求和输入稀疏性的灵活性。

Method: (1) 高度并行加速3×3卷积；(2) 为脉冲、神经元状态和权重设计统一数据存储器；(3) 低开销脉冲检测的高效脉冲图处理。

Result: 在GlobalFoundries 22nm FDSOI工艺上实现，能效达0.375 pJ/SOP，较现有最优水平提升4倍。

Conclusion: Mega架构有效解决了SNN加速器中并行性利用不足和灵活性欠缺的问题，显著提升了能效。

Abstract: Convolutional Spiking Neural Networks (SNN) offer the potential for highly energy-efficient vision processing by exploiting sparse, event-driven computation. However, existing SNN accelerators underutilize the inherent parallelism of convolutional layers and lack the flexibility to accommodate varying memory demands and input sparsity across layers. This paper presents Mega, a digital architecture for convolutional SNNs that addresses these limitations through three key contributions: (1) highly parallel acceleration of $3 \times 3$ convolutions, (2) a unified data memory for spikes, neuron states, and weights, and (3) efficient spike map processing with low-overhead spike detection. Fabricated in GlobalFoundries 22 nm FDSOI technology, Mega achieves an energy efficiency of 0.375 pJ/SOP, improving the state of the art by $4\times$.

</details>


### [447] [RQP: Resource-Oriented Quantiser Pruning for Neural Networks on FPGAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30382)
*Changhong Li,Biswajit Basu,Shreejith Shanker*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了一种资源导向的一次性量化剪枝方法，通过双向beta调度微调，在喷注子结构分类任务上相比标准HGQ工作流将搜索成本降低20.58倍，同时保持有竞争力的Pareto前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准HGQ工作流采用单调不可逆的剪枝过程，计算密集且可能忽略给定资源水平下的最优子网络。

Method: 提出资源导向的一次性量化剪枝方法，先将网络直接逼近目标搜索空间，再利用双向beta调度进行微调，以实现对Pareto前沿的更精细扫描。

Result: 在喷注子结构分类任务上，搜索成本降低达20.58倍，同时获得了有竞争力的Pareto前沿和最终网络配置。

Conclusion: 所提方法在显著降低搜索成本的同时，能够达到与标准HGQ工作流相当的性能表现。

Abstract: High granularity quantisation (HGQ) exploits weight-level quantisation and pruning to design resource-efficient neural network accelerators, achieving an attractive trade-off between accuracy and hardware utilisation. HGQ is particularly well suited to FPGA-based edge neural network applications. Standard HGQ workflow starts from a high-precision model and progressively reduces bit width, guided by gradient-based optimisation to outline the Pareto frontier. This monotonic and irreversible pruning process is computationally intensive and can overlook the optimal subnetwork for a given resource level. We propose a resource-oriented one-shot quantiser pruning method that brings the network directly close to the target search space, and then use bidirectional beta scheduling for fine-tuning to enable a more refined scan of the Pareto frontier. Validated on the jet substructure classification, JSC, task, our method reduces the search cost by up to 20.58x compared with monotonic resource reduction in standard HGQ workflows, while achieving a competitive Pareto frontier and final network configuration.

</details>


### [448] [COSM: A Cooperative Scheduling Framework for Concurrent PIM and CPU Execution on Mobile Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.30553)
*Yilong Zhao,Fangxin Liu,Onur Mutlu,Mingyu Gao,Jian Liu,Haibing Guan,Li Jiang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出COSM，一种面向移动平台的PIM与CPU协同调度框架，通过低干扰PIM控制接口和空闲感知调度方法，在共享内存空间下实现大语言模型与移动应用的高效并发执行，PIM吞吐量提升最高2.8倍，CPU性能损失低于2.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动端大语言模型部署需要兼顾隐私和低延迟，Processing-in-Memory（PIM）因其能减少高能耗数据传输而成为有效方案，但由于DRAM成本限制、电路板物理空间有限以及应用与LLM的交互，CPU和PIM必须在共享内存空间内并发运行，而银行冲突和总线拥塞等问题会削弱PIM的性能和能效优势，亟需一种协同调度方案来解决这些挑战。

Method: COSM包含两个核心创新：1）低干扰PIM控制接口，在最大化生成PIM命令的同时不干扰CPU内存访问；2）空闲感知调度方法，将PIM命令集成到CPU访问序列中的可用空闲时间窗口内，从而将PIM执行延迟对CPU隐藏，并实现PIM执行与数据传输的重叠。

Result: 在LLM与移动应用及计算密集型内核的并发执行实验中，COSM相比基线调度方法将PIM吞吐量提升最高达2.8倍，同时CPU性能损失低于2.0%。

Conclusion: COSM框架有效解决了移动平台上PIM与CPU在共享内存空间并发运行时的银行冲突和总线拥塞问题，实现了PIM与CPU的高效协同，为移动端LLM部署提供了一种低开销的加速方案。

Abstract: The development of on-device large language models (LLMs) is driven by the need for privacy and fast response times. Energy-intensive data transfer on mobile devices makes Processing-in-Memory (PIM) an effective solution. Due to stringent DRAM cost constraints, limited physical footprint on circuit boards, and the interaction between applications and LLMs, it is imperative for the CPU and PIM to operate concurrently within a shared memory space. However, challenges such as bank conflicts and bus congestion can arise, potentially diminishing the performance and energy benefits of PIM. To address this challenge, we introduce COSM, a cooperative scheduling framework designed to facilitate the concurrent operation of PIM and CPU tasks on mobile platforms. Our key innovations include: 1) a low-interference PIM control interface that generates the maximum number of PIM commands without disrupting CPU memory accesses; 2) an idleness-aware scheduling method that integrates PIM commands into available idle time windows within the CPU's access sequence. COSM not only hides PIM execution latency from the CPU, but also overlaps PIM execution with data transfer. Experiments on concurrent execution of LLMs and mobile workloads, including mobile applications and compute-intensive kernels, demonstrate that COSM improves PIM throughput by up to 2.8x compared to the baseline scheduling method with less than 2.0% CPU performance loss.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [449] [KernelSight-LM: A Kernel-Level LLM Inference Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.28565)
*Xiteng Yao,Taeho Kim,Hengzhi Pei,Xinle Liu,Kyle Ulrich,Leonard Lausen,Ashish Khetan,Xiang Song,George Karypis,Martin Herbordt*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: KernelSight-LM是一个细粒度推理模拟器，通过将服务步骤分解为带学习效率项的roofline内核模型、通信模型和主机开销模型，结合离散事件调度器，实现跨GPU代际和单GPU目标测量的两层级延迟预测，分别达到12.1%和3.8%的内核级误差，以及端到端15.4%/12.8%/3.0%和14.3%/6.2%/2.7%的TTFT/TPOT/吞吐量误差，支持硬件/软件协同设计和容量规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM进入生产服务后，需要快速评估不同硬件、模型和服务参数下的推理性能，但端到端行为耦合了服务层策略、底层GPU内核执行和快速演进的架构，导致部署特定的基准测试速度慢且难以推广。

Method: 将每个服务步骤分解为带学习效率项的roofline内核模型、通信模型和主机开销模型，通过离散事件调度器组合，并捕获前缀缓存和连续批处理等机制。提供跨代际（无需目标GPU数据）和目标测量（需一次微基准扫描）两层级预测。

Result: 跨代际层级：内核级误差12.1%（较roofline基线22.0%提升1.8x），端到端TTFT/TPOT/吞吐量误差15.4%/12.8%/3.0%。目标测量层级：内核级误差3.8%（较基线27.7%提升7.3x），端到端误差14.3%/6.2%/2.7%。

Conclusion: KernelSight-LM以远少于先前系统的目标GPU数据，达到与专用性能分析工具匹配的精度，其内核级瓶颈分解支持硬件/软件协同设计和容量规划。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) move into production serving, practitioners must rapidly evaluate inference performance across diverse hardware, models, and serving parameters to meet cost and latency targets. However, the end-to-end behavior of LLMs couples serving-layer policies with low-level GPU kernel execution and rapidly evolving architectures, forcing slow, deployment-specific benchmarking that is hard to generalize.
  We present KernelSight-LM, a fine-grained inference simulator that models token-level execution and produces kernel-level latency breakdowns. It decomposes each serving step into a roofline kernel model with a learned efficiency term, a communication model, and a host-overhead model, composed through a discrete-event scheduler that also captures mechanisms like prefix caching and continuous batching.
  KernelSight-LM offers two prediction tiers that trade target-GPU data for accuracy. The cross-generation tier uses no target-GPU measurements, only hardware specifications and kernel microbenchmarks from previously profiled GPUs, and predicts per-kernel latency on an unseen GPU generation to 12.1% error, a 1.8x improvement over the roofline baseline (22.0%).
  A second target-measured tier adds one model-agnostic kernel-microbenchmark sweep on the target GPU, sharpening per-kernel error to 3.8%, a 7.3x improvement over a comparable baseline (27.7%). Both tiers require far less target-GPU data than the prior systems they extend.
  In our simulator, these predictions yield end-to-end median (p50) errors across six model families of 15.4%, 12.8%, and 3.0% (TTFT, TPOT, throughput) in the cross-generation tier and 14.3%, 6.2%, and 2.7% in the target-measured tier, matching dedicated profiling tools while collecting far less on-device data. Beyond prediction, its kernel-level bottleneck breakdowns support hardware/software co-design and capacity planning.

</details>
